## 1. Payments Analyst

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/7975721`
- **Location:** LOCATION (also: India Locations)
- **Department:** 4138 SDC - Tech Ops
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=7975721

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies from the world's largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone's reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
As Stripe's user base and global footprint grows dramatically, we face uniquely complex operational challenges at the intersection of payments infrastructure, financial data integrity, and global scale. The Stripe Delivery Center (SDC) provides operational leverage across Stripe's portfolio supporting external users and internal teams with precision and speed.
What you’ll do
As a Technical Operations Analyst on the Payments team, you will own the reliability and accuracy of some of the world's most critical payment flows, a platform processing hundreds of billions of dollars annually. You will sit at the intersection of engineering, finance, and operations: triaging live payment failures, driving reconciliation accuracy, and partnering with financial institutions globally to resolve issues fast.
This is not a passive monitoring role. You will be expected to independently own investigations end-to-end, identify systemic issues before they escalate, and drive process improvements that scale with Stripe's growth.
Responsibilities
Own reconciliation and payments-related investigations, ensuring accurate ledgering of funds and resolving discrepancies with banking partners swiftly and precisely.
Proactively monitor payment performance across users and payment methods; triage degradation alerts, diagnose root causes, and communicate findings clearly to technical and non-technical stakeholders.
Serve as the escalation point for complex support inquiries from Technical Account Managers, Product Support, and internal Stripe teams providing fast, well-reasoned resolutions.
Interface directly with Stripe's global financial partners (card networks, banks, payment processors) to manage operational incidents and drive resolution.
Validate the accuracy of Stripe's financial data through rigorous analysis and cross-functional collaboration with finance and infrastructure engineering teams.
Identify process inefficiencies and lead automation initiatives to reduce manual workload and improve operational throughput at scale.
Build and maintain high-quality SOPs, runbooks, and process documentation to ensure institutional knowledge is captured and transferable.
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
3-7 years of experience in a technical operations, payments operations, or financial technology role.
Advanced SQL proficiency - you write complex queries (joins, window functions, aggregations) independently and use them as a primary tool for investigation and root cause analysis.
Deep understanding of end-to-end payment flows - including authorization, clearing, settlement, reconciliation, and exception handling across multiple payment methods and geographies.
Demonstrated experience investigating and resolving high-severity operational incidents in a payments or financial systems environment with minimal oversight.
Strong experience working with large-scale financial datasets - validating accuracy, identifying anomalies, and reconciling discrepancies across systems.
Proven ability to manage complex, cross-functional workflows - coordinating simultaneously with engineering, finance, banking partners, and business teams.
Track record of identifying process gaps and driving automation or efficiency improvements that meaningfully reduced manual workload.
Experience writing and maintaining SOPs, runbooks, and technical documentation to a high standard.
Excellent written and verbal communication in English - able to translate complex technical issues into clear, concise updates for both technical and non-technical audiences.
Comfortable operating with high autonomy in a fast-paced environment, managing competing priorities and time-sensitive deadlines without close supervision.
Preferred qualifications
Hands-on experience working directly with card networks, acquiring banks, or payment processors, including managing operational escalations and SLA-driven communications.
Experience with payment cost optimization
Deep understanding of end-to-end payment flows - including authorization, clearing, settlement, reconciliation, and exception handling across multiple payment methods and geographies.
Familiarity with accounting and financial reconciliation principles
Prior experience in a high-growth fintech or global payments company operating at significant transaction scale.
Experience building or improving monitoring and alerting frameworks for payment systems.
Practical history of developing AI-driven solutions designed to automate and optimize high-volume operational workflows.

## 2. Staff Security Compliance Analyst

- **Key:** `greenhouse/okta/8333434`
- **Location:** Tokyo, Japan (also: Tokyo)
- **Department:** Sec - GRC-775
- **Link:** https://www.okta.com/company/careers/opportunity/8333434?gh_jid=8333434

Secure Every Identity, from AI to Human
Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era. This work requires a relentless drive to solve complex challenges with real-world stakes. We are looking for builders and owners who operate with speed and urgency and execute with excellence.
This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
The Security Compliance team’s mission is to strengthen Okta’s position as the leading Identity-as-a-Service solution through a security-first approach to compliance. We are looking for someone with a strong compliance background and experience in the technical implementation of security controls from ISMAP, IRAP Protected, SOC2, ISO 27001, and other international frameworks. The right candidate will have participated in a mature ISMS program, delivered ISMAP compliance, and will play a key role in providing customers with confidence in Okta’s leadership in the identity space. As a Staff Security Compliance Analyst, you will support security initiatives by engaging various process owners in the design, documentation, implementation, monitoring of the appropriate controls in our computing environments, and demonstrating those controls to external auditors. In addition you will plan and lead the private sector compliance initiatives.
The ideal candidate will have hands-on experience with the technical implementation of ISMAP, SOC2 and ISO controls in a cloud-based environment using tools such as Salesforce, Okta, ServiceNow, JIRA and others. This position requires a unique set of skills including project management, and an eye towards future standards and regulations that will impact our customers. If you’re a self-starter who wants to make a difference in global cloud security, we want you on board.
Job Duties and Responsibilities:
Participate in Security Compliance Assessments of the company’s computing environment, with focus on security controls
Facilitate Okta’s ISMAP compliance assessment
Interpret requirements across multiple compliance frameworks
Collaborate to manage and maintain common controls framework for Okta
Scheduling compliance walkthroughs
Ensuring consistent operations and communications across compliance teams
Ensuring visibility into key compliance projects through reporting and metrics
Analyze gaps between current status and future compliance framework needs
Perform controls testing and develop recommendations based on confirmed observations
Work with process and control owners to help them understand the audit results, identify remediation options, and prioritize their closure
As needed, develop appropriate security documentation, including system security plans, information security policies, and risk assessment procedures
Assess security impact on changes to the systems and applications
Identify opportunities for improvement within the Compliance program and build plans to address them
Knowledge, Skills, and Abilities:
5-7+ years of experience in Information Technology or similar, preferably in a Security Governance, Risk, or Compliance (GRC) focused role
Comprehensive understanding of IT Security practices and the Governance, Risk, and Compliance lifecycle
Fluent in English and Japanese
Experience at ISMAP compliant organization(s), or performing ISMAP assessments
Familiarity with Japanese cybersecurity laws and regulations
Knowledge in IT security frameworks and best practices, such as NIST publications, FedRAMP, ISO 27001, CCM, IRAP Protected, ISMAP, BSI-C5, TISAX, SOX, HIPAA, ENS, HDS, and SOC2 Trust Principles and Criteria
Understanding of IT methodologies, such as software development lifecycle and operations
Strong analytical and problem-solving skills and the ability to “think-out-of-the-box”
Strong oral, written and presentation communication skills
Able to work independently or with a team
Bachelor’s degree or higher in Computer Science, Management Information Systems or Accounting, or equivalent experience

## 3. Content Marketing - Manager / Senior Manager

- **Key:** `lever/veeva/8f15dceb-3827-4f41-99d5-53d77b4f550c`
- **Location:** New York - New York City
- **Department:** Marketing
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/veeva/8f15dceb-3827-4f41-99d5-53d77b4f550c

Veeva Systems is a mission-driven organization and pioneer in industry cloud, helping life sciences companies bring therapies to patients faster. As one of the fastest-growing SaaS companies in history, we surpassed $3B in revenue in our last fiscal year with extensive growth potential ahead.
At the heart of Veeva are our values: Do the Right Thing, Customer Success, Employee Success, and Speed. We're not just any public company – we made history in 2021 by becoming a public benefit corporation (PBC), legally bound to balancing the interests of customers, employees, society, and investors.
As a Work Anywhere company, we support your flexibility to work from home or in the office, so you can thrive in your ideal environment.
Join us in transforming the life sciences industry, committed to making a positive impact on its customers, employees, and communities.
The Role
We are looking for an exceptional content marketing talent to drive content strategy and execution for our high-growth, market-leading enterprise cloud software platform for life sciences. This role will create thought leadership, product-level, and customer content in close collaboration with global marketing, strategy, and sales teams to deliver impactful, high-quality content across channels and audiences.
The successful candidate is an exceptional writer, strategic thinker, and project manager with a knack for breaking down complex topics into compelling, digestible content and experiences. Working closely with key partners across marketing, this individual will play a critical role in helping us drive brand awareness, affinity, and conversion through the strategic use of content and messaging.
What You’ll Do
Partner with subject matter experts to develop original content in a variety of formats, from blog posts and articles to white papers, infographics, videos, and more
Maximize the value and reach of our content by turning high-performing assets into a library of derivative pieces for use in multiple channels
Build and execute content strategies that support and amplify our short- and long-term marketing initiatives and campaigns
Collaborate closely with product marketing, customer marketing, and field marketing to map, optimize, and execute content across the buyer journey
Be a steward of best practices in messaging, grammar, writing, and style
Measure and report on content performance; optimize based on results
Contribute to standards, processes, and tools to drive greater efficiencies in how we work and operate as a cross-functional team
Stay on top of content marketing trends and bring new ideas for us to explore and test
Requirements
3+ years product marketing and/or content marketing experience in B2B tech/enterprise software space; 5+ for sr. manager
Demonstrated success leading, supporting, and executing integrated content strategy across the customer journey
Strong portfolio of published work that demonstrates exceptional writing skills and ability to translate complex messaging into simple, digestible content
A thinker and a doer mindset — excited about driving strategy while simultaneously rolling up your sleeves to write and edit content in a variety of formats
Ability to multi-task and prioritize projects in a dynamic, fast-paced environment
Collaborative team player with great interpersonal skills and the ability to lead and influence projects cross-functionally
Proven ability to build relationships with other teams and across all levels
Self-motivated, innovative, collaborative, creative, and analytical
Fast learner, detail-oriented, and must enjoy fast-paced work environments
Ability to deliver feedback thoughtfully and receive it gracefully
Strong project management skills and attention to detail
Curiosity and interest in life sciences
Bachelor’s degree
Nice to Have
Familiarity with the life sciences industry
Experience working with remote and global teams
Success bringing innovative B2B offerings to market
Interviewing with Veeva
We value your time and believe in a transparent hiring process. Here is the process you can expect.
Follow the application process and submit your resume.
Within 3 days, you will receive a link to a personality assessment administered by a third party, unless you have already completed it with Veeva previously.
Once you complete the assessment, our team will review your full application package and follow up via email with our decision.
If moving to the interview stage, the process is as follows:
A conversation with the hiring manager
A practical case exercise
A final conversation with our group's Senior Leader.
Once all interviews are complete, the manager will be in touch with a final decision.
Perks & Benefits
Medical, dental, vision, and basic life insurance
Flexible PTO and company paid holidays
Retirement programs
1% charitable giving program
Compensation
Base pay: $75,000 - $150,000
The salary range listed here has been provided to comply with local regulations and represents a potential base salary range for this role. Please note that actual salaries may vary within the range above or below, depending on experience and location. We look at compensation for each individual and base our offer on your unique qualifications, experience, and expected contributions. This position may also be eligible for other types of compensation in addition to base salary, such as variable bonus and/or stock bonus.

## 4. Internal Audit - Operational Audit Lead, EMEA

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/8205278`
- **Location:** Dublin (also: Dublin, Dublin, Ireland)
- **Department:** 6480 Internal Audit
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=8205278

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
To advance Stripe’s important mission, we are building a world class Internal Audit (IA) team. Our purpose is to strengthen the organization’s ability to create, protect, and sustain value by providing the board and management with independent, risk-based, and objective assurance, advice, insight, and foresight. We are consumed with the goal of moving as fast as the business, being powered by technology, and increasing the maturity of Stripe’s controls where it matters the most.
Our IA team is responsible for providing objective assurance on the design and operational effectiveness of Stripe’s internal controls and business processes, its compliance with laws and regulations, its risk management framework, and other governance processes. Organized as a single global function, the team operates under a unified methodology, set of policies, and governance model — with specialized leads who bring deep domain expertise while working in alignment with that shared framework. We’re looking for a leader with extensive finance, operations, fiduciary, and regulatory compliance audit experience who will help us build and scale a global audit program.
What you’ll do
As a senior member of the EMEA IA team you will be central to shaping our audit landscape in one of the most dynamic sectors of FinTech. In this pivotal role, you will help drive the execution of a comprehensive, risk-based internal audit strategy that not only anticipates emerging risks but also aligns seamlessly with our management's vision and regulatory landscape.
As a member of the Internal Audit team, you will harness your expertise in governance, risk management, and internal controls to produce invaluable assessments that empower our Board, Audit Committee, and senior leadership to make informed, strategic decisions. This is more than just an auditing position; it's an opportunity to influence the very framework of our operations, protect our organization from unforeseen challenges, and bolster our commitment to excellence and compliance in the rapidly evolving world of Fintech. If you're looking to make a significant impact and elevate your career in an innovative and supportive environment, this is your chance to step into a role where your insights will drive meaningful change and ensure our continued success.
This position is office-based in Dublin, Ireland.
Responsibilities
Lead and execute risk-based internal audits and provide input into a risk-based internal audit strategy that ensures comprehensive, end-to-end audit coverage. This strategy should reflect emerging risks, management priorities, and regulatory obligations.
Oversee evaluation and testing of governance, risk management, internal controls, compliance, financial reporting, and operational processes to ensure their effectiveness, accuracy, and regulatory compliance.
Present independent, objective reports directly to senior leadership on the effectiveness of internal controls, risk management, and governance processes, communicating complex technical and regulatory risks to senior stakeholders in clear, actionable terms.
Ability to engage with partners and regulators; help prepare and present audit evidence and findings to enhance confidence in our internal oversight processes.
Clearly communicate complex risks and impacts to senior stakeholders, including the Board, Audit Committee, and regulators (e.g., FCA, CBI).
Partner with senior management and cross-functional teams - including engineering, product, treasury, legal, compliance, and operations - to translate audit findings into durable, structured remediation plans with control enhancements, tracking all corrective actions to verified closure.
Invest in understanding the business to better identify areas of need and opportunities to advise.
Research and stay current on applicable regulatory requirements and (e.g., GAAP, IFRS, PSD2, EMD2, EBA, etc.), emerging trends and best practices.
Who you are
We’re looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
3+ years of experience in internal auditing, risk management, or compliance, preferably within the banking or financial services sector.
Expertise in auditing financial controls, operations and regulatory compliance.
Bachelor’s degree in Accounting, Finance, Business Administration, or related field.
Strong experience in risk-based audit planning, execution, and reporting across governance, compliance, financial reporting, and operational controls.
Professional certification such as CIA, CPA, CAMS, CRCM or similar is required (or active pursuit).
Strong analytical skills with experience using data analytics and continuous monitoring tools to support audit activities.
Excellent written and verbal communication skills; ability to present complex issues clearly to non-technical senior stakeholders.
Proven ability to lead cross-functional remediation efforts and track closure of corrective actions.
High degree of integrity, independence, and professional skepticism.
Preferred qualifications
Master’s degree in Accounting, Finance, MBA, or related advanced degree.
Prior experience in a regulated financial institution or network with complex relationships among network partners.
Experience with audit management software, automation platforms, and advanced data analytics.
Demonstrated track record of building continuous audit/monitoring programs.

## 5. Risk & Monitoring Analyst IV

Ready to do the most impactful work of your career? At Coinbase, we are uncompromising on our mission to increase economic freedom. The bar is high, the environment is intense, and we like it that way. This isn't a place for complacency, it’s a place to be pushed past your perceived limits. If you're ready to build the future of finance alongside people who refuse to settle for "good enough," you belong here. Coinbase is a remote-first, but not remote-only company. Expect to get together quarterly for intense in-person working sessions called “surges.” Learn more about working at Coinbase.
As a Risk & Monitoring Analyst IV on the Operational Risk and Controls (ORC) team within Global Financial Crimes Operations (GFCO), you'll own quality assurance reviews of TMS investigations that support Coinbase's AML/CFT program. You'll strengthen consistency in case execution, surface procedural gaps, and partner across Operations, Compliance, Analytics, and Learning & Development to turn quality insights into measurable operational improvements.
What you'll do:
Review TMS cases against established QA rubrics, including core sampling for regulatory filings (SARs) and risk-based sampling across priority compliance areas.
Lead calibration sessions, office hours, and structured analyst support to drive consistency and deepen understanding of complex procedural requirements.
Conduct root cause analysis on thematic quality issues and translate findings into actionable recommendations for process, training, and procedural updates.
Partner with second line of defense stakeholders and deliver timely, well-supported responses to review and audit findings.
Build and present weekly metrics, dashboards, and trend analyses that highlight risks, performance themes, and improvement opportunities to senior stakeholders.
Train new joiners, validate practice cases, and provide structured feedback to accelerate analyst readiness and quality performance.
Required Skills and Experience:
6+ years of experience in quality assurance, quality control, audit, or financial crime investigations within a regulated financial services environment.
Working knowledge of BSA/AML laws, regulations, and requirements, with demonstrated ability to apply risk-based judgment when reviewing complex investigations.
ACAMS certification or equivalent AML qualification.
Proven experience producing compliance metrics, dashboards, and performance reporting for operational and compliance stakeholders (proficiency in Google Sheets/Excel and Google Slides/PowerPoint).
Track record of presenting QA findings, root cause analyses, and procedural recommendations to senior compliance and operations leaders across multiple regions.
Utilizes generative AI responsibly, maintaining human oversight to deliver business-ready outputs and drive measurable improvements in workflow efficiency, cost, and quality.

## 6. Risk Operations Manager

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/8248096`

About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
Partner Risk Operations(PRO) is responsible for managing the risk relationships with our critical payment ecosystem partners, including global payment methods, card networks, and banking partners. Our mission is to establish operational controls that mitigate user and partner risk while enabling strategic development opportunities. At the Stripe Delivery Center, the team will provide operational support for scaled investigations, assist with inquiries, and assess nuanced and complex partner programs.
What you’ll do
In this role, you will lead a team of Partner Risk Operations Associates dedicated to managing higher-tier support, including escalations from Tier 1 and internal escalations. Your team will assess onboarding possibilities from a partner perspective, collaborating closely with our Go-To-Market (GTM) teams to ensure seamless integration of new users. As subject matter experts in card monitoring programs and banking partner relationships, you will drive initiatives that enhance our risk management capabilities.
The Partner Risk Operations Manager will be an adaptable leader and cultivate the engagement of their team members while guiding them to be the best they can be, through feedback, coaching, mentoring, and advocacy within the organization. This means helping to set team goals, and using metrics to efficiently measure and guide team performance in pursuit of those goals. To be a fit, you will have a strong operations mindset, be able to move quickly, and be passionate about delivering an incredible user experience.
Responsibilities
Recruit, manage, coach, and develop a team of in-office Risk Operations Associates
Guide your team to handle risk-related user escalations and support internal teams with complex inquiries, guiding cross-functional teams on how to navigate network compliance rules
Drive strong operational delivery and process improvement helping to maintain risk detection strategies and business rules that balance risk and customer experience
Build a great culture and ensure team members are happy, effective, and growing in their career
Set clear goals and direction, and provide regular feedback on team members’ performance
Be data-driven in your analysis of performance, and in your decision making
Transmit and foster our values, serving as a beacon of Stripe’s user-centric philosophy and culture of transparency, empathy, inclusion, and empowerment
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
At least 5+ years of direct people management experience, either in a risk or operations function
Ability to partner effectively with internal globally distributed stakeholders
Operational background including new process launches / improvements and service delivery
Problem-solving skills and comfort operating without clear guidelines
Skill and credibility doing the core work of a user facing team with a high bar for quality, and a willingness to lead by example
Excellent written and verbal communication skills in both English and Spanish
Preferred qualifications
Experience in payments, e-commerce, or fintech
Experience in building and scaling new teams from zero
Experience operating in a high growth technology company

## 7. Paid Social & Programmatic Analyst

- **Key:** `greenhouse/seatgeek/8180389`

SeatGeek believes live events are powerful experiences that unite humans. With our technological savvy and fan-first attitude we’re simplifying and modernizing the ticketing industry.
Social platforms are a critical component of SeatGeek's marketing strategy, because we need to meet live event fans where they are. As an Analyst on the Paid Social and Programmatic team, you'll play an integral role in growing a channel that spends eight figures annually across Meta, TikTok, Reddit and Snapchat. You'll bring the inherent excitement and uniqueness of live events to fans in a way that has a direct impact on SeatGeek's overall user and revenue growth.
You'll use both sides of your brain. You'll develop growth marketing strategies alongside designers, data scientists and engineers, then prove the value of those strategies through rigorous, hypothesis-led measurement. This is a channel where we run real incrementality studies rather than taking platform-reported numbers at face value, so you'll be involved in building for, and measuring, success.
What you'll do
Day-to-day management of paid social campaigns across Meta, TikTok, Reddit and Snapchat
Manage variable budgets to maximize scale while hitting ROAS targets, leaning in when performance beats target and pulling back when it doesn't
Build and optimize per-performer campaigns for artists, teams and tours against a constant onsale calendar
Run our dynamic and catalog-driven campaigns, working with product feeds and dynamic creative templates to keep thousands of live events served accurately
Design and execute experiments and A/B tests across targeting, creative, copy and landing pages
Contribute to incrementality and conversion lift studies with our Analytics team, helping design holdouts, interpret reads, and translate results into how we set budgets
Partner with our Creative team on briefs and creative testing, and close the loop with performance results
Work with Engineering on the internal tooling that automates campaign creation, helping define requirements and spot where automation is falling short
Engage directly with our platform partners at Meta, TikTok, Reddit and Snap to push their capabilities toward our priorities rather than theirs
What you have
Bachelor's degree
2+ years of hands-on experience running performance advertising, with meaningful time on Meta and at least one other major social platform
Experience managing variable, performance-led budgets at scale, where spend flexes against a ROAS or efficiency target rather than being fixed upfront
An analytical, data-driven background with a proven ability to pull insight and narrative out of large datasets
Strong project and time management skills, so you can hold many campaigns and requests at once without dropping them
Strong verbal and written communication. You're at ease explaining a complex strategy to people who don't live in the ad platforms
Comfort with a data toolbox: spreadsheets plus a BI or analytics tool such as Looker or Hex
Curiosity about measurement. You've asked whether a campaign was actually incremental, not just whether the platform reported a good number
Interest in live entertainment and sports. Knowing who's hot right now is a genuine advantage in this job
Perks
Discretionary annual bonus
Flexible work environment, allowing you to work as many days a week in the office as you’d like or 100% remotely
A WFH stipend to support your home office setup
Unlimited PTO
Up to 16 weeks of fully-paid family leave
401(k) matching
Student loan matching program
Health, vision, dental, and life insurance
Up to $25k towards family building, reproductive health services and Gender-affirming care
$500 per year for wellness expenses
Subscriptions to Headspace (meditation), Headspace Care (therapy), and One Medical
$360 per quarter to spend on tickets to live events
Annual subscription to Spotify, Apple Music, or Amazon music
The salary range for this role is $63,000 - $92,000 USD. In addition, you may receive a discretionary annual bonus based on individual and company performance. Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies - from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups - use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone's reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
The Finance Operations (FinOps) team provides and enhances core business services that are the backbone for Stripe. FinOps uses data and operational excellence to deliver technology solutions, procurement, billing and payroll services, strategic sourcing, and supplier relationship management to Stripe.
What you’ll do
We're looking for an Accounts Receivable (AR) Specialist to join our growing team. The AR Specialist will own billing and collections programs for Stripe’s growing mix of products that are invoiced. This role is responsible for projects within the end‑to‑end billing cycle, reconciliation of billing outputs to Finance reporting, and supporting AR collections strategy and improvement. The role works closely with Finance, Sales/GTM, Product, Data/Engineering, and Accounting to ensure invoices are accurate, timely, and collected.
Responsibilities
Own and execute defined Accounts Receivable (AR) programs and projects from planning through delivery, establishing scope, milestones, success measures, and accountability.
Develop and maintain project plans, track dependencies, and coordinate cross-functional execution to deliver agreed outcomes on time.
Translate AR business priorities into actionable workstreams, aligning stakeholders on requirements, deliverables, and ownership.
Proactively identify project risks, resolve execution blockers, and escalate decisions or trade-offs with clear recommendations.
Analyze AR performance and financial trends to identify gaps, investigate root causes, and recommend targeted improvements.
Partner with Finance, Sales/GTM, Engineering, and other stakeholders to deliver initiatives that improve receivables performance and the customer experience.
Lead process improvement projects that standardize and simplify AR workflows, reduce manual effort, and strengthen controls.
Gather business requirements and coordinate the prioritization, testing, and rollout of enhancements in partnership with technical teams.
Drive change management for assigned initiatives, including stakeholder communications, documentation, adoption planning, and post-launch measurement.
Define and monitor program metrics and leading indicators to measure business performance, delivery progress, and realized benefits.
Provide concise updates to AR and Finance leadership on program health, milestones, risks, decisions, and results.
Maintain program documentation, decision logs, and ownership handoffs to support sustainable outcomes and continuous improvement.
Own and execute subsidiary AR integration projects, aligning stakeholders on requirements, milestones, and dependencies while driving process improvements to standardize workflows, strengthen controls, and improve scalability.
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
8+ years of relevant experience in billing/accounts receivable with usage-based billing platforms or complex SaaS billing systems.
Strong Excel/Sheets and SQL literacy; comfortable reading billing query outputs and reconciling to invoices.
Experience working with APIs, simple python scripts, or Retool automations preferred.
Familiarity with payment methods (ACH, wires, crypto/USDC, Stripe payment links) and cash application best practices.
Excellent attention to detail, organized, and able to manage a heavy month‑end cadence and ad hoc adjustments.
Strong communication skills and ability to partner cross‑functionally (Sales, Finance, Data, Product, Legal).
A proven ability to build strong collaborative working relationships with business partners
A proven ability to be a self starter capable of driving business results without significant supervision
The ability to maintain high performance under tight timelines and a “roll up your sleeves” mindset to get any job done
The ability to bring structure to ambiguous areas of opportunity and thrive in an unstructured, fast-moving environment
Excellent problem-solving skills and demonstrated ability to work independently, analyze problems and data sets to make complex investigation decisions
Demonstrated history of taking on various types of challenging projects and producing results
Solution-oriented mindset with enthusiasm for establishing best practices
Self-disciplined, diligent, proactive and detail oriented
Preferred qualifications
Experience working at a high-growth technology or financial services company
Prior exposure to charge-through and pass‑through third‑party fees and complex fee constructs (platform fees, per txn, bps/% pricing, minimums).
Experience working at or supporting fintech or payments companies, especially crypto/stablecoin or cross‑border FX rails
Experience with Databricks, Snowflake, or similar data stacks.

## 10. Marketing Analytics - Data Analyst - July 2027 Start Date - ADP

- **Key:** `lever/veeva/28c47d34-3ad6-4485-85a9-686b4239b9ea`
- **Location:** New York - New York City
- **Department:** Generation Veeva
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/veeva/28c47d34-3ad6-4485-85a9-686b4239b9ea

Veeva Systems is building the industry cloud for Life Sciences to help companies work in a more efficient and connected way. Learn more about our products, vision and values, and status as a public benefit corporation on our website.
Crossix is positioned at the center of big data, innovative technology, media, and life sciences. Our mission is to empower life sciences companies with the data and insights they need to drive patient and provider marketing effectiveness and, ultimately, better patient outcomes. As part of Veeva, the Crossix team offers the agility of a high-growth team with the stability of a global leader.
The Role
We are hiring recent university graduates to grow the next generation of data analysts through our Analytics Development Program.
The Marketing Data Analyst (MDA) is a member of the internal analytics team focused on product analytics. This team’s primary function is to produce the analytical reports that are incorporated into Veeva Crossix products and shared with customers.
What this role is (and isn’t):
What it IS: A data analytics role. You will be part of our internal analytics team, focusing on backend report generation, quality control, and pressure-testing results. Using SQL, Excel, and our proprietary coding language, you will link marketing data to health data, transforming large datasets into core analytical reports embedded in Veeva Crossix products. Your work will be used to generate the insights our customers use to make business decisions.
What it IS NOT: This is not a client-facing consulting role. If you want to spend your days in external client meetings, presenting slide decks, and advising on marketing strategies, check out the Client Services Associate track. This is also not a software engineering role - your focus is on data logic, manipulation, and insights, not building software architecture.
This role is part of Generation Veeva, and as such you can expect:
A “learn by doing” environment with challenging and impactful work, where you will be expected to learn quickly and deliver early
A collaborative work environment where you will work closely with many levels of our team, from other early-career professionals through senior leaders
Mentorship and continuous professional development opportunities, aiding in your immediate and long-term success at Veeva
The anticipated start date is July 2027.
The Analytics Development Program offers two distinct paths. Please review our other track, the Client Services Associate role, to choose the best fit for your skills and interests.
We welcome you to apply to all roles that align with your skills and interests. See all open opportunities here: Generation Veeva Careers
What You'll Do
Produce Analytics Reports: Own the end-to-end execution, rigorous quality control, and timely delivery of high-value, recurring analytical reports across a large portfolio.
Link Complex Datasets: Connect marketing exposure data to massive, transactional healthcare datasets. Your data manipulation will provide the analytical foundation that helps pharmaceutical and life sciences companies optimize their marketing efforts and ultimately improve patient outcomes.
Formulate Analytical Hypotheses: Transform complex client questions into hypotheses that are testable using SQL, Excel, and Crossix’s proprietary analytical coding language.
Partner Internally with Client Services: Communicate the technical results and methodology of marketing campaign analyses to our Client Services teams, acting as the subject-matter expert on the underlying data logic.
Master Modern Data Tools: Leverage a robust toolkit including SQL, advanced Excel, data visualization suites, and internal platforms to clean, manipulate, and present data effectively.
REQUIREMENTS | We are looking for graduates who meet the following requirements:
Technical Foundation: Intermediate proficiency in SQL, Python, or a comparable coding language. You have a natural comfort with data structures and a knack for logical, step-by-step problem-solving.
High Drive & Performance: A self-starter with a track record of excellence who thrives in a demanding and dynamic environment, and is hungry for rapid professional growth.
Meticulous Attention to Detail: Exceptional precision and a commitment to rigorous quality control. You take pride in catching small errors before they impact the final product.
Effective Communication Skills: Strong verbal and written communication skills, with the ability to effectively share technical insights and methodology with our internal client services teams.
Academic Excellence: Bachelor’s degree (any major) with a 3.0 – 4.0 GPA, demonstrating the intellectual curiosity, time management, and work ethic necessary for a high-performance analytics career.
Experience Level: This role is designed for upcoming or recent graduates with 0–2 years of post-grad full-time work experience.
Location & Presence: You live within about a 45-60 minute commute of our NYC office (or plan to be by your start date) and look forward to collaborating with the team on-site 4 days a week.
Unwavering Integrity: Veeva is a Public Benefit Corporation (PBC) and a “do the right thing” company. We hold ourselves to high ethical standards and expect honesty and integrity from every team member.
THE PROCESS | Our process is different from most. It is designed to be fast, efficient and respectful. Here are the steps:
Application & Assessment: You submit your resume, responses to a few short application questions, and complete a brief online assessment, unless you have already completed it with Veeva previously.
Application Response (Within 1 Week): We review every application thoroughly. Within about one week of submission, we will notify you via email whether we’d like to move you to the next stage.
Initial Interview: You will participate in a 60-minute job interview with a senior member of our team.
Case Interview: If we proceed following your initial interview, you will complete an analytics case exercise. This will be a live demonstration of your skills, with no prework required.
Final Decision (Within 1 Week): Within about one week of completing your case exercise, we will either extend an official offer or let you know that we are not moving forward.
Your Decision (2 Weeks): If extended an offer, you will have two weeks to make your final decision. If you accept, we will hold a spot for you and expect you to show up on your start date. Accepting an offer and continuing to interview for other roles would be an ethical violation in our view.
Perks & Benefits
Medical, dental, vision, and basic life insurance
Flexible PTO and company paid holidays
Retirement programs
1% charitable giving program
Compensation
Starting base pay: $82,500
Work Authorization: Qualified candidates must be legally authorized to be employed in the United States. Veeva does not provide sponsorship for employment visa status (e.g., H-1B, OPT, or TN status) for this employment position.
#LI-Entry
#WU

If you need assistance or accommodation due to a disability or special need when applying for a role or in our recruitment process, please contact us at talent_accommodations@veeva.com.

About Us
Abacus Insights is transforming how data and AI work for health plans. Our mission is simple: make healthcare data usable, so the people responsible for care and cost decisions can act faster, with confidence.
As the AI and Data Platform for Payer Intelligence, we help health plans break down data silos to create a single, trusted data foundation. That foundation powers better decisions—so plans can improve outcomes, reduce waste, and deliver better experiences for members and providers alike. Backed by $100M from top investors, we're tackling big challenges in an industry that's ready for change. Our platform enables GenAI use cases by delivering clean, connected, and reliable healthcare data to support automation, prioritization, and decision workflows—and it's why we are leading the way.
Our innovation begins with people. We are bold, curious, and collaborative—because the best ideas come from working together. We embrace the thoughtful use of AI and automation to drive innovation and efficiency, and we look for individuals who are curious and adaptable—those excited to leverage emerging technologies to enhance how we work—while keeping human insight, connection, and our clients at the center of every decision.
Ready to make an impact? Join us and let's build the future together.
About the Role
We are seeking a Senior Business Intelligence Analyst (Healthcare) to join our Growth Analytics team. This role is ideal for a data professional with 4–5 years of experience who is passionate about turning healthcare data into decisions that drive revenue.
You will be the analytics engine behind our go-to-market motion. You will build the AI-assisted dashboards, forecasts and models that tell leadership where the pipeline stands, where margin is made, and where the next opportunity is. You will also provide hands-on sales operations support to the Sales Director team.
Success in this role looks like dashboards and analysis that leaders rely on, a trusted reputation as the go-to partner for sales and growth analytics, and the ability to turn complex healthcare data into clear, compelling narratives. You will thrive if you bring curiosity, learning agility and a proactive approach in a fast-paced, high-growth environment.
Your Day to Day
Growth analytics and business intelligence
Design, build and maintain AI-assisted dashboards, KPIs and executive reporting in BI tools (Power BI or Tableau), SQL and Excel to track pipeline, bookings, revenue and sales performance
Use AI and agentic tools to accelerate analysis, automate recurring reporting and data preparation, and generate insights and narrative summaries, applying human judgment to validate accuracy before anything reaches leadership
Analyze pipeline, historical and real-time data to surface trends, develop forecasts, and inform pipeline and revenue planning
Build revenue, cost and deal-margin models, and support pricing analysis for our healthcare data products
Prepare the data, analytics and demo materials that showcase our payment integrity, medical cost management, value-based care, population health, risk adjustment and HEDIS/Star offerings
Provide data and analysis for RFPs and proposals so that every number holds up to scrutiny
Track payer, market, competitor and regulatory trends, and translate findings into actionable insights for Sales, Product and executive leadership
Ensure data accuracy and consistency across systems, reports and dashboards
Sales operations support
Maintain CRM documentation and data hygiene, and troubleshoot CRM and tooling issues
Support weekly sales calls and pipeline reviews
Assist the Sales Director team with demo environments, sales collateral, and ad hoc deal and account analytics
Create executive presentations and sales decks with clear, data-driven narratives
Partner cross-functionally with Sales, Finance and GTM teams on analysis and strategy
What You Bring to the Team
4–5 years of relevant experience in analytics, business intelligence, consulting or sales operations
2–3+ years working in US healthcare or healthcare data/analytics (payer, provider, vendor, health-tech or consulting)
Strong SQL skills and hands-on experience building dashboards in a BI tool such as Power BI or Tableau
Hands-on experience using AI and agentic AI tools (such as Claude, ChatGPT or Copilot) to build, automate or accelerate analytics and reporting work, with sound judgment about validating AI-generated output
Advanced Excel proficiency
Baseline financial and business acumen: comfortable with unit economics, margin, pricing and financial reporting
Ability to synthesize data into insights and executive-ready communication, both written and verbal
Demonstrated learning agility, problem-solving ability and attention to detail
Bachelor’s degree in Business, Economics, Analytics or a related field
What We Would Like to See, but Not Required
Familiarity with payer claims data, healthcare financial concepts or payment integrity
Experience reporting from a CRM such as Salesforce or HubSpot
Experience building or configuring AI agents, automated workflows or integrations (for example via MCP, APIs or low-code tools)
Exposure to cloud data platforms and modern data stack tools
What You’ll Get in Return
Competitive leave and benefits
Comprehensive health coverage
Equity for every employee – share in our success
Growth-focused environment – your development matters here
Work Arrangements
Standard hours: 9 hours/day, 5 days/week
Location: Remote; must be able to work U.S. hours (Eastern Time)
Shift: This role requires alignment with U.S. business hours (Eastern Time) to support the Growth Analytics and Sales leadership teams in real time. Specific working hours may vary based on business needs.
Our Commitment as an Equal Opportunity Employer
As a mission-led technology company helping to drive better healthcare outcomes, Abacus Insights believes that the best innovation and value we can bring to our customers comes from diverse ideas, thoughts, experiences, and perspectives. Therefore, we dedicate resources to building diverse teams and providing equal employment opportunities to all applicants. Abacus prohibits discrimination and harassment regarding race, color, religion, age, sex, national origin, disability status, genetics, protected veteran status, sexual orientation, gender identity or expression, or any other characteristic protected by federal, state, or local laws.
At the heart of who we are is a commitment to continuously and intentionally building an inclusive culture—one that empowers every team member across the globe to do their best work and bring their authentic selves. We carry that same commitment into our hiring process, aiming to create an interview experience where you feel comfortable and confident showcasing your strengths. If there’s anything we can do to support that—big or small—please let us know.
AI Use in Recruitment
We use AI-powered tools throughout our recruitment process. This includes Greenhouse's Real Talent Matching Technology, which helps prioritize applications based on job-related criteria, as well as additional AI tools used to support sourcing, interview preparation, and other recruiting tasks. We do not remove the human element—our recruiters and hiring teams review all applications and make all decisions related to candidate progression and hiring, regardless of which tools are used to support that process.
Identity and Location Verification
To protect the integrity of our hiring process, all interviews are conducted by live video with your camera on. Virtual or blurred backgrounds and headsets are not permitted absent an approved accommodation, and you should be in a well-lit, private space where your face and surroundings are clearly visible. ## 16. Staff Backend Engineer, Supportability Decisions

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/8261010`
- **Location:** Dublin (also: Ireland Locations)
- **Department:** 8217 Risk Engineering
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=8261010

About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies, from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups, use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
Supportability sits at the vital intersection of platform safety, regulatory trust and merchant growth. Our mission is to ensure legitimate businesses can seamlessly onboard and thrive on Stripe, while proactively safeguarding the financial ecosystem from bad actors and high-risk activity.
The Supportability Decisions team is a newly forming pillar anchored in our Dublin office. In this role, you will be the foundational technical lead establishing this capability in Dublin. Our mission is to accelerate Stripe’s global expansion by building the canonical Supportability Decision Layer; a stateful distributed platform that provides unified, real-time, and explainable risk determinations to unlock fast, compliant merchant onboarding and payment method expansion worldwide.
Doing this at scale requires solving high-stakes risk challenges with precision. Deceptive merchant activity, such as transaction laundering, introduces significant threats as bad actors actively adapt to mask illicit and unsupported activities behind legitimate storefronts. Rather than relying on rigid, fragmented controls that create unnecessary friction for good users, our team builds the intelligent decision systems that systematically separate legitimate businesses from adversarial behavior, enabling Stripe to grow safely and aggressively.
What you’ll do
As a Staff Backend Engineer and Technical Lead for Supportability Decisions, you will take ownership of defining and building this new space from the ground up. You will build systems that operate at the intersection of AI/ML, distributed data processing and real-time risk decisioning. You will tackle high-ambiguity technical challenges in an adversarial threat landscape, turning broad risk policies and operational needs into resilient, high-throughput backend services and stateful decision engines. As the technical anchor in Dublin, you will set architectural patterns, mentor engineers as the team scales from its initial members to ~7 ICs, and drive technical alignment with distributed partner teams globally.
Responsibilities
Lead the technical vision, system architecture and delivery of the canonical Supportability Decision Layer, establishing stateful, explainable decision APIs consumed across automated systems and human operations.
Architect decision aggregation and AI/ML orchestration pipelines, synthesizing upstream machine learning outputs, website intelligence, backsite detection, and agentic AI evaluations into unified, low-latency determinations.
Design high-throughput, low-latency distributed backend services that balance platform safety, regulatory compliance, and minimal friction for legitimate merchants.
Navigate ambiguous, zero-to-one problem spaces to define clean domain models, contracts and interfaces across transaction laundering detection, web-presence screening and Local Payment Method (LPM) support.
Build resilient, AI-assisted decision engines designed to withstand circumvention, ensuring systems maintain integrity against sophisticated bad actors who actively evolve their tactics.
Serve as the technical lead and mentor for the Dublin engineering team, setting architectural patterns, conducting design reviews, and raising the technical bar as the team expands.
Partner closely with Engineering Managers, Product Managers, Risk Operations, and Data Science to translate strategic risk policy into scalable technical roadmaps.
Drive technical alignment and system integrations across global partner teams, leveraging strong written design artifacts and asynchronous collaboration to ensure cohesive platform architecture.
Who you are
We're looking for a technical leader who thrives in ambiguity, has deep distributed systems instincts, and is energized by building a critical platform that enables global merchant growth while safeguarding the financial ecosystem. If that sounds like you, we encourage you to apply.
Minimum requirements
10+ years of professional backend software engineering experience building and maintaining complex distributed systems in production.
Proven track record as a technical lead or staff engineer driving multi-quarter technical initiatives from ambiguous requirements to production delivery.
Deep experience designing durable, scalable APIs, data models, and backend architectures capable of processing mission-critical data with high reliability.
Strong communication and leadership skills with experience mentoring engineers, aligning cross-functional stakeholders, and driving technical consensus across distributed teams.
Fluency in a modern backend language (such as Ruby, Java, Go, Python, or C++).
Preferred qualifications
Experience building systems in trust, safety, or risk domains where external bad actors actively evolve their tactics (e.g., transaction laundering, fraud, deceptive practices, or platform abuse).
Experience architecting workflow orchestration, policy evaluation, or decision platforms that balance robust risk evaluation with frictionless merchant onboarding and product access.
Familiarity with global payment ecosystems, Local Payment Methods (LPMs), financial partner dynamics, or card network standards.
Background in AI/ML systems, agentic workflows, orchestrators, or integrating machine learning models into high-throughput, real-time backend decision pipelines
Prior experience serving as an anchor or founding engineer establishing a new team or technical domain in a regional engineering hub.

Who we are:
About Stripe:
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world's largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone's reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About Bridge:
Bridge, a Stripe company, makes it easy for businesses to move, store, and accept money using stablecoins. Through Bridge's APIs, companies can send and receive payments across fiat rails and multiple blockchains, launch and manage their own branded stablecoins, hold balances in custodial wallets, and issue cards that spend directly from stablecoin balances. Stripe acquired Bridge in February 2025, and together we're building the financial infrastructure for a world where money moves at internet speed.
As part of our Bridge Fraud Operations team in Mexico City, you'll join a young and growing team at the center of this work. You'll help protect a fast-growing stablecoin platform, investigate new and evolving fraud patterns, improve existing processes, build new ones, and bring fresh ideas to how the team works.
About The Team
At Stripe, our Mexico City office is a vibrant hub at the forefront of our mission to reshape the financial landscape for businesses worldwide. Our team prioritizes collaboration, innovation, and excellence. As a member of the Mexico City team, you'll be part of a mission-driven community dedicated to enhancing the global economy and increasing the GDP of the internet. We strive for excellence by creating with craft and beauty, while having fun and celebrating our successes together.
We cultivate a culture of collaboration, inclusivity, and support where every team member's voice matters. Our commitment shines through as we handle over a million support cases each year, empowering our users not just to solve problems but to achieve their goals.
What you'll do
In this role, you will implement and manage User Policy operational processes that support responsible market growth and instill trust among users and partners. You will assess risks and opportunities, liaise with financial partners and merchants, and manage escalations while providing timely responses. As a User Policy Specialist at Stripe, you will apply and enforce the rules and requirements that determine which businesses can use our platform. This role is closely aligned with Stripe's mission, as it involves making key decisions about business eligibility to access our economic infrastructure.
Responsibilities
• Assess and identify potential risks associated with business partnerships, making informed decisions that balance opportunity with compliance to protect Stripe's brand and reputation.
• Investigate, action, and triage escalations across multiple channels, while providing accurate and timely responses to stakeholders.
• Collaborate with various stakeholders, including business units, legal teams, and financial partners, to communicate User Policy requirements and ensure alignment with Stripe's mission and value proposition.
• Utilize analytics to optimize strategies that can be enforced across Stripe's portfolio.
• Develop and implement User Policy operational processes that ensure compliance with internal rules and external regulations, thereby enabling responsible business growth and market expansion for Stripe.
• Identify gaps and areas for improvement within current User Policy frameworks, recommending enhancements to support sustainable business practices and progressive growth.
• Participate in periodic business reviews to evaluate the impact of User Policy on Stripe's market performance and contribute to strategic discussions aimed at enhancing Stripe's growth and reputation.
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
• You possess experience in risk analysis, investigations, risk operations, compliance, customer facing roles or any related operative background
• You have excellent English communication skills, both written and verbal
• You excel in diagnostic thinking and problem-solving, with experience using data to make assessments and judgments
• You enjoy working in an in-office environment with strong cross-team collaboration and support
• You are able to prioritize and enjoy working in a quick-moving environment
• You are humble and have a proven track record for working well across teams and with external partners
• You're willing to periodically work a weekend (remotely) for which you will be compensated with weekdays off
• You are available to work 100% in-office on Av. Paseo de la Reforma, Mexico City. We're open to people relocating from other Mexican states, and you may be eligible for relocation assistance from Stripe, depending on the distance you'd be moving from.
Preferred qualifications
• Risk experience in payments, Start-Up's, e-commerce, or fintech mitigating digital and card-not-present risk
• Experience in Trust and Safety related roles
• Knowledge of risk schemes, risk prevention methods, and risk detection tools
• Prior risk experience across multiple industries and verticals
• Experience in a high-growth technology company
• Experience working from a queue

## 20. Enterprise Risk Management Analyst

- **Link:** https://www.coinbase.com/careers/positions/8233945?gh_jid=8233945

Ready to do the most impactful work of your career? At Coinbase, we are uncompromising on our mission to increase economic freedom. The bar is high, the environment is intense, and we like it that way. This isn't a place for complacency, it’s a place to be pushed past your perceived limits. If you're ready to build the future of finance alongside people who refuse to settle for "good enough," you belong here. Coinbase is a remote-first, but not remote-only company. Expect to get together quarterly for intense in-person working sessions called “surges.” Learn more about working at Coinbase.
Coinbase's Enterprise Risk Management team helps the company identify, understand, and manage risks that could affect our strategy, operations, and customers. As an Enterprise Risk Management Analyst, you'll sit at the intersection of risk, data, and strategy, supporting core ERM activities while using data and technology to make them more insightful and scalable. You'll analyze risk information, identify emerging risk patterns and signals, improve GRC data quality, support risk tooling and automation, and translate analysis into clear insights that help leaders make better risk-informed decisions.
What you'll do:
Analyze large, complex, and sometimes unstructured datasets to identify trends, anomalies, correlations, concentrations, and emerging enterprise risk signals.
Build repeatable analytical methodologies, dashboards, metrics, and visualizations that improve how Coinbase understands and monitors enterprise risk.
Connect information across risk assessments, incidents, KRIs, audit findings, regulatory developments, business performance, and external signals to develop an integrated view of risk.
Investigate changes in risk indicators, challenge assumptions, identify underlying drivers, and distinguish meaningful signals from noise.
Automate recurring analysis, data preparation, and reporting using SQL, Python, AI, and other available technologies.
Partner across Finance, Legal, Compliance, Security, Operations, Strategy, and regional teams to understand evolving risks and translate complex analytical findings into concise, executive-ready narratives and recommendations.
Required Skills and Experience:
1-4 years of experience in risk analytics, data analytics, business intelligence, financial analysis, or a related analytics discipline, with demonstrated ability to extract, clean, join, and analyze complex datasets.
Proficiency in SQL and working experience with Python or comparable analytical tools for data manipulation and analysis.
Demonstrated ability to move from an ambiguous business question to a structured analytical approach and actionable conclusion, including independently investigating unexpected results.
Ability to translate analytical findings into clear risk insights for leadership and cross-functional stakeholders, connecting how findings relate to enterprise-wide risk, opportunities, or decisions.
Utilizes generative AI responsibly, maintaining human oversight to deliver business-ready outputs and drive measurable improvements in workflow efficiency, cost, and quality.

## 21. Associate Technical Consultant - Entry-Level Consulting

- **Key:** `lever/veeva/5bfdcc48-ca36-4a7f-b99d-c14dd1453c0a`
- **Location:** Poland - Wroclaw
- **Department:** Generation Veeva
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/veeva/5bfdcc48-ca36-4a7f-b99d-c14dd1453c0a

Do you love technology but want to do more than just code? If you are looking for meaningful work with a big impact and a chance to work directly with stakeholders to design solutions and solve complex problems, then this role is for you.
The Technical Consultant Development Program (CDP) is an accelerated pathway that transforms early-career professionals into high-impact technical consultants—empowering you to write production code, build custom integrations, and deploy enterprise cloud and AI solutions that help global Life Sciences companies bring life-changing therapies to patients.
Explore More Generation Veeva Roles
We welcome you to apply to all roles that align with your skills and interests. See all open opportunities here: Generation Veeva Careers
The Role
As a customer-facing Technical Consultant, you will build, customize, and deploy Veeva’s industry-leading enterprise SaaS platforms and AI solutions directly to customers. Moving beyond theoretical advisory, this hands-on role gives you immediate ownership to translate complex business challenges into production-ready software systems for customers in Life Sciences. We provide comprehensive domain and technical training at the start of the program, making this an ideal accelerator for high-potential graduates eager to build a high-impact career at the intersection of enterprise cloud architecture and client-facing technical services.
* Now hiring for our April 2027 and September 2027 start dates.
The Program
As part of the Technical Consultant Development Program, you will:
Ramp up quickly. Your journey begins with an intensive, engineering-focused onboarding program designed to build deep technical expertise in Veeva’s Vault Platform, APIs, and custom development—while equipping you with the core consulting and customer-facing skills needed to engage directly with clients and ship production code from day one.
Learn by doing. Skip the sidelines and routine QA—you’ll take on real engineering responsibility within your first three months, writing production code, building custom integrations, and working directly with customers to solve complex technical challenges.
Thrive together. Learn side-by-side with seasoned Technical and Migration Architects who prioritize your growth, guiding you through complex system designs and providing transparent, actionable feedback on your code and client delivery.
Build your future. Fast-track your career from Associate to Consultant in less than two years, building a powerful foundation in full-stack implementation, DevOps, and technical consulting while leading complex architecture projects alongside a global team.
What You'll Do
Build solutions. Skip basic QA and debugging to write production code, develop custom integrations using Java SDKs and APIs, build DevOps pipelines, and deploy Veeva AI tools on the Vault Platform.
Architect and migrate. Work directly with migration architects to design custom backend logic, configure enterprise SaaS platforms, and execute complex data migration scripts for global life sciences clients.
Ship internal tooling. Accelerate delivery workflows by developing cutting-edge, developer-facing tools used globally across the Veeva Services engineering team.
Own technical delivery. Take on real architectural responsibility from day one, mastering software engineering and client-facing technical strategy to advance from Associate to Consultant in under two years.
What We Are Looking For
Education: Bachelor's degree in engineering, computer science, or related field with strong technical background.
Technical Foundation: Demonstrated experience in Object-Oriented Programming.
Communication: Excellent technical storytelling skills; you enjoy explaining why a specific technical architecture or decision is right for a customer
Drive: Thrive in a challenging, intense environment that rewards initiative and rapid learning.
Analytical Mindset: Strong critical thinking skills and proven track record of academic performance.
Integrity: Veeva is a Public Benefit Corporation (PBC) and a “do the right thing” company; we expect that from all employees.
Work Ethic: A commitment to excellence and passion for working hard to achieve results.
Travel: Willingness to travel as needed (up to 20% of the time).
Experience: Recent university graduate
NICE TO HAVE
Languages: Experience with Python, C, HTML, Java, JavaScript, or SQL.
Cloud: Familiarity with cloud platforms (AWS/Azure).
AI Tools: Experience using AI-powered development tools or CLI assistants (e.g., Claude Code, Gemini CLI, or similar).
THE PROCESS | Our process is different from most. It is designed to be fast, efficient and respectful. Here are the steps:
Application: Submit your resume, short cover letter, and take a personality test, unless you have already completed it with Veeva previously.
Initial Response: Within one week, we will notify you of your next step via email.
Interview: The next step is a 60-minute interview with a Veeva leader,
Technical Challenge: A 30-minute online technical challenge delivered via Coderpad.
Case: After the coding challenge, there is a case exercise with a member of our services team. You will have three days to prepare for the case exercise.
Decision: Within one week, we will extend an offer or let you know that we are not moving forward.
Commitment: You will have two weeks to accept your offer. We value commitment; once you accept, we will hold a spot for you and expect you to join us on your start date. Accepting an offer and continuing to interview would be an ethical violation in our view.
Compensation
Starting Salary: 120,000 ZL
After one year, if you perform well, you will receive a compensation increase and participate in our bonus program
Work Authorization: Qualified candidates must be legally authorized to be employed in the European Union. Veeva does not provide sponsorship for employment visa status for this employment position.
Work Environment: Veeva is a Work Anywhere company. Associate Consultants work remotely for Veeva in Poland. To take advantage of regular opportunities for in-person collaboration, skill development, and socialization, we require that you live within a 60 minute commute of Krakow, Warsaw, or Wroclaw.
