## 47. Financial Operations Analyst

- **Key:** `lever/zoox/b8eefee3-f699-4cc6-881c-0a4b739b50d0`
- **Location:** Foster City, CA
- **Department:** Finance, Places and Build
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/zoox/b8eefee3-f699-4cc6-881c-0a4b739b50d0

The Finance Operations team at Zoox sits at the center of the Finance organization. The team consolidates planning inputs from across FP&A into a single companywide financial forecast, and reports actuals and forecast performance upward to Zoox leadership and our parent company, Amazon. Finance Operations co-owns MEC activities with Zoox Accounting, driving core accrual, allocation, and reconciliation workstreams, as well as key managerial financial reporting packages. Lastly, Finance Operations governs purchase order hygiene, enforcing accounting policy at the point of commitment and tracking timely goods receipts.
As a Finance Operations Analyst, your primary responsibilities will focus on MEC and purchase order hygiene, and flex into projects supporting other areas of scope for the team. You will partner with budget owners across the organization, the FP&A analysts, and Accounting on these workstreams and directly drive improvement of financial processes and accuracy of financial results.
This role requires a solid understanding of U.S. GAAP and procure-to-pay processes, along with the judgment to know when to resolve an issue directly and when to escalate it. You will leverage SAP S4, SAP Ariba, and other internal reporting systems to analyze spend data and provide actionable insights that improve compliance, spend visibility, and the efficiency of the requisition process.
In this role you will:
Support month-end close by providing PO and receipt data to Accounting and identifying unreceived goods and services for accrual
Build and maintain trackers on committed spend versus budget by cost center
Track and report compliance metrics to Finance leadership, including PR turnaround time, coding error rates, and policy exception volume
Review purchase requisitions for compliance with U.S. GAAP and Zoox accounting policies
Conduct pre-approval compliance checks of purchase orders against budget and forecast models, escalating discrepancies to the appropriate lead analyst within FP&A
Conduct Forecast vs. Actuals (FvA) analysis during monthly and quarterly closes
Review agreements and contracts to determine appropriate PR structure and coding, and re-structure PRs and POs as policies change
Partner with business owners to resolve incomplete or non-compliant purchase requisitions, and guide requesters and the internal team on PR requirements and accounting policy
Own open PO review and cleanup, including aging analysis, closure of stale purchase orders, and truing up open POs with requesters
Qualifications
BA/BS degree, preferably in Finance, Accounting, or a quantitative field
4+ years of relevant work experience in finance operations, procurement, accounting, or FP&A
Solid understanding of U.S. GAAP, particularly as it applies to expense classification and capitalization
Hands-on experience with a major ERP system (e.g., SAP, Oracle, NetSuite)
Experience with spend management or procurement software (e.g., Coupa, Ariba)
Advanced proficiency in Microsoft Excel (e.g., pivot tables, lookups, and analysis of large datasets)
Strong interpersonal and communication skills, with the ability to explain accounting policy clearly to non-finance stakeholders
Detail-oriented and reliable, with a positive attitude and the ability to quickly adapt to changes in a fast-moving environment
Bonus Qualifications
Public accounting experience, Big 4 a huge plus
High-growth startup and/or public company accounting experience
Experience with manufacturing and/or fulfillment accounting
Experience supporting a procure-to-pay system implementation or an accounting policy rollout

## 48. Corporate Accounting Analyst (Bangalore)

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world's largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone's reach while doing the most important work of your career.
To further this important mission, we're building our Accounting team in Bangalore Finance and are looking for someone to help us scale for the future in a fast-paced environment.
Role Summary
We are seeking a highly detail-oriented and tech-forward Corporate Accounting Analyst to join our fast-paced corporate accounting team. In this role, you will play a critical part in managing General Ledger (GL) activities, ensuring accurate close processes, and driving accounting precision across specialized technical areas, including debt, prepaids, intercompany transactions, and lease accounting .
The ideal candidate holds a Chartered Accountant (CA) qualification, possesses 4–6 years of progressive accounting experience, and brings a modern, tech-enabled mindset—specifically the ability to leverage AI tools, automation, and advanced analytics to streamline workflows and solve complex accounting problems in a rapidly evolving environment.
Key Responsibilities
Own end-to-end accounting schedules, journal entries, and balance sheet reconciliations for corporate accounting areas .
Manage month-end, quarter-end, and year-end closing activities within prescribed timelines.
Conduct variance analyses on P&L and Balance Sheet line items, investigating unexpected fluctuations and providing actionable commentary to management.
Support internal and external audit requests by preparing audit schedules, supporting documentation, and clear accounting workpapers.
Collaborate with cross-functional teams (F&S, FinOps, Tax, Operations) to ensure accurate accounting treatment for complex transactions.
Leverage ERP automation, analytical capabilities and AI-driven tools (e.g., prompt engineering, automated reconciliation workflows, intelligent data extraction) to enhance accuracy and timeliness.
Identify bottlenecks in the financial close cycle and implement automated solutions or streamlined workflows.
Utilize advanced data tools to process large volumes of financial data accurately under tight deadliness.
Required Qualifications & Experience
Education & Certification: Chartered Accountant (CA) or equivalent professional accounting designation is mandatory.
Experience: 4 to 6 years of post-qualification core corporate accounting experience, with strong hands-on expertise in General Ledger operations.
AI & Tech Proficiency: Demonstrated ability to adopt and operate AI tools (e.g., generative AI for documentation/analysis, workflow automation software, advanced ERP modules) to optimize accounting tasks.
Key Competencies & Soft Skills
Fast-Paced Adaptability: Ability to prioritize effectively, meet tight monthly/quarterly closing deadlines, and thrive in an agile, dynamic environment.
Analytical & Detail-Oriented: Exceptional problem-solving skills with a high degree of precision in numerical and technical accounting work.
Communication: Clear verbal and written communication skills to effectively collaborate with remote teams, management, and external auditors.
Self-Starter: Strong ownership mindset, able to work independently as a contractor with minimal supervision.
Nice to Have
Knowledge of (or experience in) the technology or payments industry
Big4 working experience
Oracle suite
Hubble and SQL

This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
The Enterprise Technology Team
The Enterprise Technology team builds and delivers the secure, scalable systems that power how our business runs. Our People Technology Team sits at the heart of this work, overseeing the full Hire-to-Retire technical ecosystem—including core applications like Workday, Greenhouse, and Cornerstone—and ensuring these platforms evolve alongside the company's growth. We partner closely with HR, Finance, Operations, and Security to bring data-driven, secure, compliant, and innovative solutions to life.
The Staff Product Analyst Opportunity
Okta is seeking a Staff Product Analyst (Workday Payroll, Absence & Time Tracking) to join our People Technology Team. This is a pivotal role dedicated to the functional optimization of Okta's global Workday platform across Payroll, Absence, and Time Tracking. You will lead the end-to-end lifecycle of these programs, from strategic discovery and design to global deployment and adoption. You will act as a trusted advisor to stakeholders across People, Payroll, IT, and Finance, ensuring our People Technology suite drives automation, accuracy, and efficiency. If you are passionate about improving the employee experience through thoughtful Workday design, you'll fit right in.
What you’ll be doing
Product Strategy and Roadmap: Collaborate on the product vision and multi-year roadmap for Workday Payroll, Absence, and Time Tracking, ensuring alignment with global People Technology and Finance team objectives.
Stakeholder Partnership: Act as a strategic partner to People, Payroll, and Finance leadership, translating complex business needs into clear functional requirements and configuration designs.
Functional Configuration and Design: Lead the design and configuration of Payroll, Absence, and Time Tracking business processes, calculations, eligibility rules, and pay components that prioritize employee experience and operational efficiency.
Lifecycle Management: Manage the full lifecycle of payroll, absence, and time tracking configuration, from initial discovery and requirements gathering through testing, deployment, and ongoing maintenance.
Data-Driven Insights: Leverage Workday reporting and analytics to monitor process performance, identify trends, and deliver actionable recommendations for payroll, absence, and time tracking optimization.
Vendor and Platform Management: Oversee relationships with third-party vendors (e.g., ADP) to ensure seamless data flows and adherence to service level agreements.
Operational Excellence: Champion continuous improvement by analyzing process and configuration issues, leading testing efforts, and proposing changes that enhance accuracy and reduce manual intervention.
Feature Adoption and Training: Drive end-user adoption of new Workday features through comprehensive documentation, user guides, and training programs.
Compliance and Security: Ensure all payroll, absence, and time tracking configurations strictly adhere to global data privacy requirements and internal security standards.
What you’ll bring to the role
Workday Expertise: 7+ years configuring Workday Payroll, Absence, and/or Time Tracking, including business process design, calculated fields, and eligibility rules.
Payroll, Absence, and Time Tracking Domain Knowledge: Deep functional expertise in Workday Payroll (GL, pay components, taxes), Absence, and Time Tracking configuration, along with global HR business processes.
Testing and Quality: Proven experience leading tenant configuration testing, including UAT and regression testing, to ensure accurate, reliable releases.
Analytical Skills: Advanced capability in building custom reports, dashboards, and hubs to drive data-informed decision-making.
Strategic Communication: Excellent interpersonal skills with the ability to influence stakeholders and collaborate effectively across global time zones.
Problem Solving: Superior ability to troubleshoot complex functional issues and design scalable configuration solutions in a fast-paced environment.
Agile Methodology: Experience working in an Agile environment, managing backlogs, and delivering value through iterative cycles.
And extra credit if you have experience in any of the following!
Experience with Workday integrations especially the Global Payroll Connect.
Workday Extend experience.
Workday Certification in Payroll, Absence, or Time Tracking.
Additional requirements:
U.S. soil status - the employee must be on U.S. soil, which means the 50 states, the District of Columbia, or outlying areas of the United States, as defined in Federal Acquisition Regulation (FAR) 2.101, and be a U.S. person.
U.S. person status - the employee must be either a U.S. citizen as defined in 42 U.S. Code § 9102; a natural person who is a lawful permanent resident as defined in 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(20) or who is a protected individual as defined by 8 U.S.C. 1324b(a)(3); or a U.S. national (i.e. includes citizens and non-citizens born in outlying possessions such as American Samoa and Swains Island), green card holders, refugees, and asylees. Working on a U.S. visa does NOT qualify as a U.S. person.

## 50. Analyst, Privacy (Privacy Assessments)

- **Key:** `greenhouse/coinbase/8281622`
- **Location:** Remote - USA (also: US - Remote Zone 1 (Job Requisitions Only))
- **Department:** Security & Privacy
- **Link:** https://www.coinbase.com/careers/positions/8281622?gh_jid=8281622

Ready to do the most impactful work of your career? At Coinbase, we are uncompromising on our mission to increase economic freedom. The bar is high, the environment is intense, and we like it that way. This isn't a place for complacency, it’s a place to be pushed past your perceived limits. If you're ready to build the future of finance alongside people who refuse to settle for "good enough," you belong here. Coinbase is a remote-first, but not remote-only company. Expect to get together quarterly for intense in-person working sessions called “surges.” Learn more about working at Coinbase.
As a Privacy Analyst on the Privacy Operations team within Security, you'll play a central role in how Coinbase identifies and manages privacy risk across its products and platforms. This team partners with Legal, Product, and Engineering to perform privacy assessments, manage privacy issues through the product lifecycle, and continuously improve the frameworks and tooling that keep customer data safe. You'll own the execution of complex privacy assessments for new products and features, drive issue remediation, and build scalable processes that embed privacy by design into how Coinbase ships.
What you'll do:
Own end-to-end privacy assessments for new products and features, synthesizing findings and driving remediation with cross-functional partners across Legal, Product, and Engineering.
Perform regulatory requirement mapping against the privacy common controls framework and identify where control updates are needed to address evolving obligations.
Track privacy requirements and issues throughout the product lifecycle, managing escalations and driving timely resolution with business owners.
Partner with Product and Engineering teams by embedding into roadmap planning processes and advising on privacy-by-design principles for onchain and web3 products.
Identify and implement automation and AI-driven process improvements to streamline privacy assessments and issue management workflows.
Required Skills and Experience:
3+ years in technology risk management with a focus on privacy or security, including hands-on experience conducting privacy assessments (DPIAs/PIAs, data flow reviews, or equivalent).
Demonstrated ability to map regulatory requirements (GDPR, CCPA, PIPEDA, ePrivacy) to operational controls and validate remediation efforts.
Proven ability to translate privacy legal guidance and product specs into functional requirements and actionable remediation plans.
Experience managing a high volume of concurrent assessments while balancing strategic priorities and stakeholder escalations across technical and non-technical audiences.
Familiarity with applying privacy principles to blockchain, onchain, and web3 products or willingness to develop this expertise rapidly.
Utilizes generative AI responsibly, maintaining human oversight to deliver business-ready outputs and drive measurable improvements in workflow efficiency, cost, and quality.

Veeva Systems is a mission-driven organization and pioneer in industry cloud, helping life sciences companies bring therapies to patients faster. As one of the fastest-growing SaaS companies in history, we surpassed $3B in revenue in our last fiscal year with extensive growth potential ahead.
At the heart of Veeva are our values: Do the Right Thing, Customer Success, Employee Success, and Speed. We're not just any public company – we made history in 2021 by becoming a public benefit corporation (PBC), legally bound to balancing the interests of customers, employees, society, and investors.
Join us in transforming the life sciences industry, committed to making a positive impact on its customers, employees, and communities.
The Role
We are seeking a talented, highly motivated and detail oriented individual to join our team. This position will join our Crossix Data Platform team and will own data analysis and quality checks to ensure high standards of our Pioneer product.
Pioneer serves as the centralized, scalable pre-analytics data hub that ingests, cleanses, and standardizes billions of raw media exposure data records (from direct publisher feeds like 1PC, site tags, and TV) into a unified data schema, enabling downstream matching against de-identified health data to measure marketing effectiveness for pharma campaigns.
This is a great opportunity for someone who is excited about taking part in building high quality data products and working closely with product and engineering teams towards successful execution.

Plan and execute user acceptance testing for Pioneer, including functional checks and data quality checks
Create and manage dashboards to track the data KPIs - volumes, breakdowns, trends, platform throughput

Identify opportunities for improving data quality to ensure data maintains high quality standard

Experience working independently on analyses, demonstrating understanding of the underlying data and business questions
Strong knowledge of SQL and scripting
Experience investigating data issues/ troubleshooting skills/ detail oriented
Bachelor's degree Statistics / Math / Engineering or other field emphasizing statistical and analytical skills
Self-motivated and fast learner with a strong sense of ownership
Strong verbal and written communication skills in English
Nice to Have
Familiarity and experience with the advertising industry
Experience working on big data platforms
Good knowledge of JIRA

About Anthropic
Anthropic’s mission is to create reliable, interpretable, and steerable AI systems. We want AI to be safe and beneficial for our users and for society as a whole. Our team is a quickly growing group of committed researchers, engineers, policy experts, and business leaders working together to build beneficial AI systems.
About the role
As a Technical Recruiter at Anthropic, you will be instrumental in shaping the future of our organization by identifying, engaging, and hiring the best and brightest minds across a range of disciplines. As we continue to push the boundaries of AI research and development, we need a passionate recruiter who can help us build a world-class team dedicated to creating safe and beneficial AI systems.
Key Responsibilities
Develop and execute strategic recruiting plans to identify and source highly qualified candidates
Partner with technical and non-technical hiring managers and interviewers to understand hiring needs, team matching, required skills and qualifications
Implement recruiting processes and programs such as developing targeted outreach campaigns, building connections with industry leaders, and removing any unfair biases from the hiring process
Collaborate with leadership and cross-functional partners to understand organizational needs and map out long-term talent acquisition strategies that balance priorities across all technical teams
Enhance Anthropic’s employer brand to showcase our mission, culture, and values to candidates
Provide an exceptional candidate experience throughout the recruiting process
Minimum qualifications
Deep full lifecycle recruiting experience, with substantial time supporting technical engineering organizations
Proficiency with an applicant tracking system like Greenhouse and other modern sourcing tools
Experience partnering directly with hiring managers on intake, bar calibration, and interview loop design
Sound independent judgment on candidate quality, and the ability to independently partner with multiple hiring managers are complex searches
Genuine interest in Anthropic's mission and in the role a strong security function plays in achieving it
Preferred qualifications
Experience recruiting at a high-growth technology, AI, or machine learning company
Working knowledge of the security credential landscape and clear judgment about where those credentials carry signal and where they do not
Experience hiring for engineering, incident response, threat intelligence, insider risk, or physical security
Experience improving recruiting processes, market maps, or interview architecture where they were thin or absent
Comfort using LLMs to accelerate sourcing, research, and market mapping
The annual compensation range for this role is listed below.
For sales roles, the range provided is the role’s On Target Earnings ("OTE") range, meaning that the range includes both the sales commissions/sales bonuses target and annual base salary for the role.
Annual Salary:
$240,000—$295,000 USD
Logistics
Minimum education: Bachelor’s degree or an equivalent combination of education, training, and/or experience
Required field of study: A field relevant to the role as demonstrated through coursework, training, or professional experience
Minimum years of experience: Years of experience required will correlate with the internal job level requirements for the position
Location-based hybrid policy: Currently, we expect all staff to be in one of our offices at least 25% of the time. However, some roles may require more time in our offices.
Visa sponsorship: We do sponsor visas! However, we aren't able to successfully sponsor visas for every role and every candidate. But if we make you an offer, we will make every reasonable effort to get you a visa, and we retain an immigration lawyer to help with this.
For roles performing work in California: We will consider for employment all qualified applicants, including those with arrest or conviction records or criminal histories, in a manner consistent with the requirements of applicable state and local laws, including the San Francisco Fair Chance Ordinance, the City of Los Angeles’ Fair Chance Initiative for Hiring Ordinance, the Los Angeles County Fair Chance Ordinance for Employers, and the California Fair Chance Act.
We encourage you to apply even if you do not believe you meet every single qualification. Not all strong candidates will meet every single qualification as listed. Research shows that people who identify as being from underrepresented groups are more prone to experiencing imposter syndrome and doubting the strength of their candidacy, so we urge you not to exclude yourself prematurely and to submit an application if you're interested in this work. We think AI systems like the ones we're building have enormous social and ethical implications. We think this makes representation even more important, and we strive to include a range of diverse perspectives on our team.
Your safety matters to us. To protect yourself from potential scams, remember that Anthropic recruiters only contact you from @anthropic.com email addresses. In some cases, we may partner with vetted recruiting agencies who will identify themselves as working on behalf of Anthropic. Be cautious of emails from other domains. Legitimate Anthropic recruiters will never ask for money, fees, or banking information before your first day. If you're ever unsure about a communication, don't click any links—visit anthropic.com/careers directly for confirmed position openings.
How we're different
We believe that the highest-impact AI research will be big science. At Anthropic we work as a single cohesive team on just a few large-scale research efforts. And we value impact — advancing our long-term goals of steerable, trustworthy AI — rather than work on smaller and more specific puzzles. We view AI research as an empirical science, which has as much in common with physics and biology as with traditional efforts in computer science. We're an extremely collaborative group, and we host frequent research discussions to ensure that we are pursuing the highest-impact work at any given time. As such, we greatly value communication skills.
The easiest way to understand our research directions is to read our recent research. This research continues many of the directions our team worked on prior to Anthropic, including: GPT-3, Circuit-Based Interpretability, Multimodal Neurons, Scaling Laws, AI & Compute, Concrete Problems in AI Safety, and Learning from Human Preferences.
Come work with us!
Anthropic is a public benefit corporation headquartered in San Francisco. We offer competitive compensation and benefits, optional equity donation matching, generous vacation and parental leave, flexible working hours, and a lovely office space in which to collaborate with colleagues. Guidance on Candidates' AI Usage: Learn about our policy for using AI in our application process.

The Sales Strategy & Operations team is responsible for the overall productivity and effectiveness of the sales organization, partnering closely with the sales team and other teams across Stripe to drive highly strategic initiatives. This team is integral in the success of our sales organization. With a commitment to excellence and a deep understanding of the art and science of sales, our team is dedicated to informing the sales strategy, optimising processes, and providing insights that propel our sales growth. We do this by being objective, trusted partners to the Sales Leadership team.
What you’ll do
We are seeking a technically sophisticated and detail oriented individual who will serve as a Sales Strategy and Operations Data Analyst in support of the AMER Velocity (Commercial and SMB) business. This person will be responsible for improving the quality of sales analytics and insights, including standardization of reporting and scalability with the primary objective of enabling sales leadership to make effective, analytically driven decisions. You will work primarily with Sales Leadership and other cross-functional business partners to drive the operational cadence and strategy/planning of the business.

Operating Cadence: Execute on a standard weekly, monthly and quarterly cadence of operations (weekly forecasting calls, monthly business reviews, quarterly business reviews).

## 60. Senior Cloud Infrastructure Engineer

- **Key:** `ashby/valon/71dcb68c-302d-4f8c-80cf-c1bd5ac106d5`
- **Location:** Remote
- **Department:** Engineering
- **Link:** https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/Valon/71dcb68c-302d-4f8c-80cf-c1bd5ac106d5

ABOUT THE COMPANY
Valon is the leading AI platform for mortgage servicing, building the AI-native operating system for regulated finance.
Before providing the system of record that powers the entire industry, we built and ran our own mortgage servicer on it. That gave us a real regulated environment to prove our technology could replace the mainframe systems servicers have used for decades. Carrington acquired that servicer and adopted ValonOS as its core platform, allowing us to focus entirely on scaling our SaaS platform.
ValonOS is now on track to power 1 in 6 U.S. mortgages across major mortgage servicers like Newrez, Carrington, and ServiceMac. Every process on the platform is structured and programmable, enabling our AI agents to perform core servicing work, like handling payments, escrow, and homeowner questions, directly within the system's controls.
We recently raised a $150 million Series D at a $2.3 billion valuation joined by new investor Ribbit Capital, with continued support from existing backer a16z, amongst others.
ENGINEERING AT VALON
Our engineering team powers a modern software ecosystem designed to disrupt one of the most outdated and heavily regulated industries in finance: mortgage servicing. We’ve built the foundation of a scalable platform capable of managing billions in assets with precision and reliability. Now we’re focused on scaling infrastructure, improving developer experience, and building high-leverage systems that support rapid product iteration and long-term business growth. We’re a tight-knit, high-agency group of engineers from Stripe, Jane Street, Meta, and Google, and we aim to do the best work of our lives—together.
Our headquarters are in New York City, with offices in San Francisco. We also fully support remote work.
ABOUT THE ROLE
We’re looking for a Senior Software Engineer to join our Cloud Infrastructure team within Platform Engineering. You’ll work on the foundational infrastructure that powers all of Valon’s products—compute, databases, storage, and networking—ensuring our platform is scalable, secure, and reliable as we grow.
This is a high-impact, high-ownership role. We handle over 100 million background jobs per day and expect to scale more than 10x over the next two years. As our commercial pipeline accelerates and we onboard major enterprise customers, the infrastructure challenges shift from feature delivery to scale, reliability, and supporting novel deployment architectures. You’ll be at the center of that work.
EXAMPLE PROJECTS ON THE ROADMAP:
- Adopt and operationalize new data stores to support our growth trajectory: Vitess, Clickhouse, Redis.
- Scale our batch processing control plane for faster component creation and fulfillment.
- Build production-equivalent sandbox environments for customer testing, demos, and onboarding.
- Support new deployment models for enterprise customers, including infrastructure configurations that meet the security and data residency requirements of top-tier financial institutions.
RESPONSIBILITIES
- Design, build, and operate core cloud infrastructure across compute, storage, databases, and networking layers.
- Own and improve the reliability, scalability, and security of Valon’s production systems as we scale to support major enterprise deployments.
- Evaluate, adopt, and operationalize new infrastructure technologies (e.g., Vitess, Clickhouse, Redis) to meet evolving product and scale requirements.
- Collaborate with product engineering, platform, and operations teams to define infrastructure solutions that unlock product innovation and operational efficiency.
- Participate in on-call rotations and incident response, driving improvements to system observability, alerting, and reliability practices.
- Contribute to architectural decisions and technical strategy for the platform, with an emphasis on pragmatic, well-reasoned trade-offs.
- Mentor teammates through code reviews, design discussions, and knowledge sharing.
IDEAL BACKGROUND
- 3–5 years of professional experience in infrastructure engineering, platform engineering, DevOps, SRE, or cloud systems.
- Hands-on experience managing and operating cloud infrastructure on AWS, GCP, or Azure at meaningful scale.
- Strong working knowledge of containerization and orchestration (Docker, Kubernetes) and infrastructure-as-code tools (Terraform, Pulumi, or similar).
- Experience with distributed systems concepts: networking, storage, load balancing, and database operations.
- Solid engineering fundamentals—you can write clean, well-tested code and contribute meaningfully to services and tooling, even if infrastructure is your primary domain.
- A product mindset: you think about how infrastructure decisions impact the teams and products that depend on your work.
- Strong communication skills and a collaborative approach to working across teams.
- Experience in fast-paced, high-growth environments is a plus. Familiarity with regulated industries (fintech, healthcare, etc.) is a bonus but not required.
BENEFITS
- Compensation: competitive salary with a meaningful stake in the company via equity, and 401k plan.
- Health & well-being: comprehensive medical, dental, and vision benefits.
- Commuter benefits: pre-tax deductions for public transportation, rideshare services, and parking expenses.
- Grow together: company-wide orientation, learning & development opportunities, and regular review cycles featuring 360-degree feedback.
- Play together: quarterly budgets for team and company outings.
- Generous time off: flexible paid time off, sick days, and 11 company holidays.
- Baby bonding time: 12 weeks off for both birthing and non-birthing parents, fully paid.

Our benefits aim to promote health and wellbeing across all areas of Palantirians’ lives. We work to continuously improve our offerings and listen to our community as we design and update them. The list below details our available benefits and some of the perks that can be enjoyed as an employee of Palantir Technologies.
Benefits
• Employees (and their eligible dependents) can enroll in medical, dental, and vision insurance as well as voluntary life insurance

Life at Palantir
We want every Palantirian to achieve their best outcomes, that’s why we celebrate individuals’ strengths, skills, and interests, from your first interview to your longterm growth, rather than rely on traditional career ladders. Paying attention to the needs of our community enables us to optimize our opportunities to grow and helps ensure many pathways to success at Palantir. Promoting health and well-being across all areas of Palantirians’ lives is just one of the ways we’re investing in our community. Learn more at Life at Palantir and note that our offerings may vary by region.
In keeping consistent with Palantir’s values and culture, we believe employees are “better together” and in-person work affords the opportunity for more creative outcomes. Therefore, we encourage employees to work from our offices to foster connectivity and innovation. Many teams do offer hybrid options (WFH a day or two a week), allowing our employees to strike the right trade-off for their personal productivity. Based on business need, there are a few roles that allow for “Remote” work on an exceptional basis. If you are applying for one of these roles, you must work from the state in which you are employed. If the posting is specified as Onsite, you are required to work from an office.
If you want to empower the world's most important institutions, you belong here. Palantir values excellence regardless of background. We are proud to be an Equal Opportunity Employer for all, including but not limited to Veterans and those with disabilities. Palantir is committed to making the application and hiring process accessible to everyone and will provide a reasonable accommodation for those living with a disability. If you need an accommodation for the application or hiring process, please reach out and let us know how we can help.
Please note that you will never be asked to submit a payment or share financial information to participate in our interview process. If you suspect that you've been contacted by a scammer, we recommend you cease all communication with the individual and consider reporting them to the relevant authorities, such as the US FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3).
If you would like to understand more about how your personal data will be processed by Palantir, please see our Privacy Policy.

## 62. Senior Data Analyst

- **Key:** `lever/veeva/398a6579-7bb0-4e63-b43a-2042d4947d56`
- **Location:** Portugal - Lisbon
- **Department:** Data Operations
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/veeva/398a6579-7bb0-4e63-b43a-2042d4947d56

Veeva Systems is a mission-driven organization and pioneer in industry cloud, helping life sciences companies bring therapies to patients faster. As one of the fastest-growing SaaS companies in history, we surpassed $3B in revenue in our last fiscal year with extensive growth potential ahead.
At the heart of Veeva are our values: Do the Right Thing, Customer Success, Employee Success, and Speed. We're not just any public company – we made history in 2021 by becoming a public benefit corporation (PBC), legally bound to balancing the interests of customers, employees, society, and investors.
As a Work Anywhere company, we support your flexibility to work from home or in the office, so you can thrive in your ideal environment.
Join us in transforming the life sciences industry, committed to making a positive impact on its customers, employees, and communities.
The Role
Veeva Link’s vision is to become the global leader for deep data in life sciences. We are looking for a detail-oriented and experienced Data Analyst to join our product team.
In this role, you will play a key part in developing and implementing a data governance framework, ensuring data accuracy, completeness, and integrity. Your main responsibilities include establishing data quality monitoring processes, identifying root causes of inconsistencies, and collaborating with cross-functional teams to resolve data gaps.
To thrive in this role, you need expertise in SQL and Python for data manipulation and automation. You should be comfortable working with large datasets, designing dashboards with actionable recommendations, and applying statistical techniques to assess and enhance data quality.
The ideal candidate has experience in creating documentation (process documentation, data collection guidelines) and in contributing to data governance policies and best practices.
What You'll Do
Implement a data governance framework
Establish and maintain robust processes for tracking and assessing data quality
Develop automated checks to proactively detect data inconsistencies
Apply statistical techniques to enhance data quality
Create customized reports and dashboards
Extract data from databases (e.g., Redshift, MongoDB)
Identify and resolve root causes of quality issues
Collaborate in cross-functional teams of product managers, engineering, and operations
Create and maintain documentation
Requirements
5+ years of work experience in data analysis or data quality role
Strong technical skills in data analysis, with proficiency in SQL and Python
Experience in data storage and processing on cloud platforms such as AWS, GCP, or Azure
Strong problem-solving skills and experience working with cross-functional teams
Experience writing process documentation and data collection guidelines
Nice to Have
University degree in medicine or life sciences
Understanding of healthcare, life sciences, or pharmaceutical industry data
Experience with data visualization tools to create dashboards and reports
Understanding of data engineering concepts (ETL, data pipelines)
Experience with workflow automation tools such as Apache Airflow
Experience working in an Agile or product-driven environment
Familiarity with data privacy and compliance regulations (e.g., GDPR, HIPAA)
Interviewing with Veeva
We value your time and believe in a transparent hiring process. Here is the process you can expect.
Follow the application process and submit your resume.
Within 3 days, you will receive a link to a personality assessment administered by a third party, unless you have already completed it with Veeva previously.
Once you complete the assessment, our team will review your full application package and follow up via email with our decision.
If moving to the interview stage, the process is as follows:
A conversation with the hiring manager
A practical case exercise
A final conversation with our group's Senior Leader.
Once all interviews are complete, the manager will be in touch with a final decision.
Perks & Benefits
1% charitable giving program
Life insurance
Pension contribution
Fitness reimbursement
Compensation
Total compensation: 48,000 - 65,000 EUR
The total compensation range listed here has been provided to comply with local regulations and represents a potential total compensation range for this role. Please note that actual total compensation may vary within the range above or below, depending on experience and location. We look at compensation for each individual and base our offer on your unique qualifications, experience, and expected contributions. This position's total compensation may be composed of other types of compensation, such as variable bonus and/or stock bonus.

## 63. Data Analyst, Intern

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/8194287`
- **Location:** Toronto (also: Toronto, Ontario, Canada)
- **Department:** 5112 General University
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=8194287

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a technology company focused on improving the conditions for economic growth and prosperity. We build programmable financial infrastructure, rethinking from first principles how financial services should work, to make it easier and cheaper for any business to start and scale. More than 10 million businesses build on Stripe, spanning the economic frontier—from solo founders to established enterprises—united by a practical focus on growth. The most ambitious companies in the world use Stripe as core infrastructure to grow faster. They process trillions of dollars a year on Stripe, equivalent to around 1.6% of global GDP. While economic growth makes everyone better off, open markets also enable greater variety. When any business can easily serve a global customer base, the quality and diversity of products in the world increase, and craft and creativity are unleashed into the smallest niches. Our own growth is wholly contingent on the success of the businesses building on Stripe. We therefore invest back into our technology at an unusual rate. We make upgrades to our products every single day to deliver compounding gains to our customers. We maintain some of the most reliable APIs on the internet. We build entirely new pieces of financial infrastructure to enable new ideas. And our significant advances in risk and fraud infrastructure over many years are making the internet economy safer and more accessible.
Though people at Stripe don’t tend to take themselves seriously, Stripe is a fairly serious place: our customers are depending on us for their livelihoods. We admire ambition, intensity, curiosity, humility, and rigor. The most effective people become knowledgeable about many domains besides their own. Any company is an applied exercise in understanding some aspect of society or the market. In working with so many (especially the new and innovative ones), we think that Stripe is one of the very best places to learn about how the world works.
About the team
Data Science at Stripe is a vibrant community where data analysts and data scientists learn and grow together. You'll work with some of the most fundamental data at Stripe, and use that data to help drive company-wide initiatives. We have a variety of Data Analytics roles and teams across Stripe and will seek to align you to the most relevant team based on your background.
What you’ll do
As an intern at Stripe you’ll work on projects across our stack that directly impact the way millions of businesses operate. You’ll own problems end to end with the support of your manager and teammates. It’s an opportunity to work alongside some of the most creative and technically rigorous data analysts and scientists in the industry. You'll partner deeply with teams across Stripe to ensure that our users, our products, and our business have the models, data products, and insights needed to make decisions and grow responsibly. You'll work closely with partners to extract insights from the rich and complex data at Stripe. You'll build metrics, scalable data pipelines, dashboards, and reports to inform and run the business. You'll deliver actionable business recommendations through analyses and data storytelling.
Our internship program is competitive and the expectations are high.
Responsibilities
Analyze data and running experiments to drive impact
Ensure that our users, our products, and our business have the models, data products, and insights needed to make decisions and grow responsibly
Create metrics, reports, and dashboards to inform and run the business
Extend Stripe’s metrics semantic layer
Build new data assets and improve existing data assets to support decision-making, products, and business processes
Assess the impact of decisions, product launches, and product improvements using business analytics and A/B testing
Learn quickly by asking great questions, finding how to work with your mentor and teammates effectively, and communicating the status of your work clearly
Present your work to the Data Science team, partner teams, and fellow interns
Who you are
Ambitious builder: You’re energized by building solutions without clear precedent and solving problems with far-reaching consequences. Successful Stripes are deeply curious, and prefer the joy of discovery to the comfort of certainty.
Rigorous thinker: You appreciate that things worth doing are rarely simple. You enjoy working on problems that have never been tackled before.
Adaptable problem solver: You adapt quickly and treat obstacles as opportunities. At Stripe we embrace kindness while encouraging Stripes to take measured risks and act boldly, even in the absence of consensus.
Minimum requirements
Enrolled in a quantitative Bachelor’s or Master's degree program (e.g. Data Analytics, Statistics, Economics, Mathematics, etc.) with the expectation of graduating in December 2027 or spring/summer 2028
Experience with SQL
Proficiency with AI tools to accelerate model development, analysis, and coding
Experience communicating and collaborating with multidisciplinary stakeholders in a team environment
Preferred qualifications
Experience writing and debugging data pipelines
Demonstrated ability to evaluate and receive feedback from mentors, peers, and stakeholders via experience from previous internships or other multi-person projects
Ability to learn new systems and form an understanding of those systems, through independent research and working with a mentor and subject matter experts

## 64. Product Sales Development Representative, China Market

- **Key:** `greenhouse/stripe/8179341`
- **Location:** Singapore (also: Singapore Locations)
- **Department:** 1136 Product Sales Development Reps
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=8179341

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world's largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone's reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
As a Sales Development Representative (SDR) on the Product China (GCN) team at Stripe, you'll support some of the region's largest and most innovative companies across various industries. You'll help power Stripe's growth engine by partnering closely with the Demand Generation and Account Executive teams to qualify leads and build a high-quality sales pipeline. You thrive on engaging with prospects, uncovering their needs, and identifying high-value opportunities. You're skilled at navigating early-stage sales conversations and energized by both learning new products and articulating their value in complex scenarios.
You also enjoy building—whether it's improving the demand generation and sales processes, sharpening how we communicate Stripe's value, or creating tools and assets that help the team scale. If you're hungry, curious, persistent, and a strong team player, we'd love to hear from you.
For the first months, you'll be part of the Sales Development Associate program, which is designed to accelerate your onboarding and ramp to full productivity as an SDR. This intensive program is built to help you quickly build and develop skills required to be successful in this role. Upon completion, you'll continue learning and growing in your career as part of Stripe's Sales Development Academy. These programs are endorsed and supported by sales leaders as an important part of investing in our people.
We take a data-driven, problem-solving approach to sales development, and look for someone who is confident in both prospecting to customers and in helping design our strategy.
What you'll do
Responsibilities
Research and create outreach materials for high-value prospects, in partnership with SDRs and AEs
Follow up with Marketing-generated leads to qualify as sales opportunities. Move solid leads through the funnel connecting them to a salesperson, and arranging meetings
Execute outbound sales plays created by Marketing
Initiate contact with potential customers through cold-calling or responding to inquiries generated from Marketing
Develop relationships with prospects to uncover needs through effective questioning to qualify interest and viability to prepare hand-off to sales
Follow up with potential customers who expressed interest but did not initially result in a sales opportunity
Effectively work through lead list meeting and exceeding service-level agreements, consistently update activity and contact information within the CRM system, and support weekly reporting efforts
Collaborate and provide feedback and insights to Marketing to help improve targeting and messaging
Prospect into enterprises of all sizes across the China region using a variety of tools and outreach methods
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
A track record of top performance or prior success
Superior verbal and written communication skills
Self-starter who is able to operate in a hyper-growth environment
High professional fluency in Mandarin Chinese and English is required for this role, as it involves working primarily with and prospecting into companies from the China market
This role requires in-office participation three (3) days per week in our Singapore office
At Stripe, we're looking for people with passion, grit, and integrity. You're encouraged to apply even if your experience doesn't precisely match the job description. Your skills and passion will stand out—and set you apart—especially if your career has taken some extraordinary twists and turns. At Stripe, we welcome diverse perspectives and people who think rigorously and aren't afraid to challenge assumptions. Join us.

## 65. Senior Site Reliability Engineer (Auth0)

- **Key:** `greenhouse/okta/7418982`
- **Location:** Barcelona, Spain (also: Spain Remote)
- **Department:** Tech Ops-610
- **Link:** https://www.okta.com/company/careers/opportunity/7418982?gh_jid=7418982

Secure Every Identity, from AI to Human
Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era. This work requires a relentless drive to solve complex challenges with real-world stakes. We are looking for builders and owners who operate with speed and urgency and execute with excellence.
This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
Auth0 provides an unparalleled authentication experience for hundreds of millions of users worldwide. Our commitment to reliability is a key foundation of our product and our dedication to exceeding customer availability expectations is a core engineering focus. As a Senior Site Reliability Engineer, you'll join our SRE team based in Europe to ensure our production systems are not only operational but also resilient, scalable, and ready for exponential growth. This isn't just about keeping the lights on; it's about directly contributing to the platform's core resiliency and robustness. You'll be a hands-on builder, crafting solutions that make our system more reliable by design.
What you’ll do:
Design and build custom software in Go to enhance the platform's reliability, resiliency, and redundancy.
Partner with engineering teams to embed reliability principles, improving the availability, performance, and observability of our services.
Use your deep understanding of infrastructure and observability principles to identify opportunities for improvement within the product and implement solutions.
Contribute to our follow-the-sun on-call rotation, providing rapid, effective response to critical incidents and using your expertise to troubleshoot, mitigate or accurately escalate production issues. Because our team is globally distributed, your on-call shifts will only occur during your standard local working hours.
Develop and refine our SRE tooling and processes, focusing on automation and operational efficiency.
Define, document, and champion reliability best practices across the organisation.
What you'll need to be successful:
This role requires a unique blend of a software engineer's mindset and operational expertise. You'll thrive in this role if you have:
A proactive and systematic approach to problem-solving, with a high degree of ownership.
Proven experience in a production environment supporting large-scale, mission-critical applications with a high degree of autonomy.
Proficiency in at least one programming language, with a preference for Go. You should be comfortable writing custom applications, not just scripts.
Experience with infrastructure as code (Terraform), container orchestration (Kubernetes, Docker) and GitOps (ArgoCD).
Demonstrable expertise in a major cloud provider (Azure, AWS, or GCP).
A strong grasp of microservices architecture, databases (SQL, NoSQL), and networking fundamentals, so you can understand how custom code can solve platform-level issues.
An understanding of core SRE principles, including SLIs, SLOs, and error budgets.
Experience in an on-call rotation for a 24/7 cloud-based environment.
Exceptional communication and collaboration skills, with a proven ability to work effectively in a remote, distributed team, where tasks may be self-driven.
We're looking for someone who is not just looking for a job, but a career-defining opportunity to tackle complex challenges at a massive scale. If you're a curious and motivated engineer who's passionate about building reliability directly into the platform, we'd love to hear from you.

## 67. AP & Expenses Projects Analyst

Secure Every Identity, from AI to Human
Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era. This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
The Opportunity
The AP & Expenses Projects Analyst will play a key role in driving operational excellence, automation, and project coordination within our Accounts Payable and Employee Expense Management functions. This role sits at the intersection of finance operations and technology, focusing on streamlining processes, identifying efficiency gaps, supporting automation initiatives, and collaborating with cross-functional and global teams.
What you’ll be doing
Process Improvement & Transformation: Identify process inefficiencies, operational gaps, and bottlenecks across AP and expense management workflows; recommend and support end-to-end continuous improvement initiatives.
Project Coordination & Documentation: Support project delivery life cycles, track milestones, gather and articulate business requirements, write process documentation (SOPs), and manage task tracking via platforms such as Jira and Confluence.
Testing & Implementation: Coordinate User Acceptance Testing (UAT), author detailed test scenarios/cases, facilitate testing cycles with operational teams, and track defect resolution through go-live.
Analytics & Business Insights: Extract, manipulate, and analyze finance data to develop reporting models, monitor key performance indicators (KPIs), and present actionable operational insights to leadership.
Stakeholder & Global Collaboration: Partner closely with regional finance operations, procurement, IT, and global business partners to align project deliverables and maintain financial controls.
What you’ll bring to the role
Education: Bachelor’s degree in Commerce, Accounting, Finance, Business Administration, Information Systems, or a related discipline.
Experience: 2 to 3.5 years of professional experience in Accounts Payable, Finance Operations, Process Improvement, Automation, or Project Coordination.
Domain Knowledge: Solid foundational understanding of Accounts Payable and Employee Expense Management processes, including invoice processing, payment runs, expense audits, reconciliations, and internal financial controls.
Data & Analytical Tools: Advanced proficiency in Microsoft Excel and Google Sheets (advanced formulas, pivot tables, data modeling, dashboards).
Methodologies & Tools: Proven familiarity with project tracking and documentation tools such as Jira and Confluence.
Interpersonal Skills: Strong problem-solving abilities, high analytical acumen, excellent verbal/written communication, and capacity to handle multiple priorities in a globally distributed setup.
And extra credit if you have experience in any of the following
Hands-on experience or exposure to ERP, Procure-to-Pay (P2P), and expense platforms such as Coupa, NetSuite, SAP, or Oracle.
Experience developing dashboards and visualizations using Business Intelligence tools like Tableau, Microsoft Power BI, or Google Workspace tools.
Hands-on scripting and automation capability utilizing Google Apps Script, Python, VBA, or RPA technologies.
Familiarity with emerging technologies, including Generative AI, AI-driven automation, Optical Character Recognition (OCR), and Intelligent Document Processing (IDP).

## 68. Regulatory Attorney

- **Key:** `lever/palantir/58367f3e-1be8-4452-9282-c0d0deb69afc`
- **Location:** London, United Kingdom
- **Department:** Legal
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/palantir/58367f3e-1be8-4452-9282-c0d0deb69afc

A World-Changing Company
Palantir builds the world’s leading software for data-driven decisions and operations. By bringing the right data to the people who need it, our platforms empower our partners to develop lifesaving drugs, forecast supply chain disruptions, locate missing children, and more.
The Role
Palantir is seeking a Regulatory Counsel to join our Compliance function within the Legal Team. Our team’s mission is to proactively provide compliance advice to the business, in an effort to support Palantir’s responsible business growth.
In this role, you’ll support teams across the global business as they navigate various regulatory and contractural requirements to ensure compliance with applicable laws across various jurisdictions and to support Palantir’s strategic business objectives. Our ideal candidate can identify novel issues within an indeterminate landscape, navigate complex issues independently, and enjoys being innovative when solving problems. In this role, you’ll draw on legal expertise, strong analytical reasoning and practical judgement to recommend appropriate solutions and work collaboratively with various stakeholders to see those solutions implemented.
We’re a team that values intrinsically motivated individual contributors that can also seamlessly work with a range of teams and stakeholders - operating solo and collaboratively. Delivering complex information using effective communication skills and building consensus using strong interpersonal skills will aid you in achieving team wins. Although this role is focused on international regulatory compliance, you should have a flexible altitude to assist with the changing needs of the business, including in the U.S. legal and regulatory compliance space and as the regulatory landscape for artificial intelligence evolves. Our ideal candidate would use a first-principles based approach to navigating novel compliance issues with a clear understanding of cross-jurisdictional enforcement risks and be able to effectively advise the business on how to best navigate and mitigate risks in the countries we operate.
Core Responsibilities
Facilitate the creation, development and roll out of international policies and processes to ensure that Palantir is complying with applicable laws and regulations in the UK, Europe, APAC, the Middle East and other non-U.S. jurisdictions where we operate
Monitor, interpret and advise on legal and regulatory requirements and developments across multiple jurisdictions, including those related to anti-bribery and anti-corruption, conflicts of interest and business ethics, sanctions, data privacy and artificial intelligence
Support the development, continuous maintenance and delivery of the compliance program in the U.K., Europe, APAC, the Middle East and other non-U.S. jurisdictions, including providing trainings, managing risk assessments, monitoring the whistleblower hotline, and conducting internal investigations
Evaluate and advise on compliance with contractual requirements, ESG requirements, legal and regulatory risks, conflicts and general compliance issues
Establish and maintain relationships with business functions based on high level confidence, and act as a key touchpoint for legal compliance queries by answering questions as well as providing business-oriented information, solutions and practical advice

## 73. Site Reliability Operations Analyst

The Role
As a Site Reliability Operations Analyst you are the engine behind Palantir deployments. You are responsible for crafting, implementing and executing processes to streamline workflows and reduce friction. You track and stabilise projects, remove roadblocks, and anticipate customer needs to free up our engineers to focus their time and attention on the technical problems they are best equipped to solve.
This position requires a combination of project management, process optimisation, and execution skills. You are a person who loves fixing problems and always embraces the best idea, even when it is not your own.
Core Responsibilities
Work on many different types of problems and challenges. You might be supporting a deployment at a large, global customer one day, and jetting off across the world to help out with a new pilot project the next.
Be the first responders when things go wrong. Even if a problem is outside your area of expertise, you make the first move to fix it, and only ask for help when we’ve exhausted all that we can possibly do.
Craft and implement process to reduce friction and enable all team members to spend their time on what they do best.
Think creatively, work collaboratively, and do whatever it takes to get the job done.
What We Value
Extraordinary judgment and composure in high-pressure situations.
A creative approach to project management centered around lightweight frameworks that enable rapid iteration and low-overhead methods of keeping our customers informed.
Proven track record of developing effective and collaborative relationships with customers.
Meticulous attention to detail, including maintaining accurate records and diligently tracking key project metrics.
Enthusiasm for working on site with customers and/or supporting internal projects and senior leadership, bringing order and efficiency to critical internal initiatives.
What We Require
Ability to travel 25-75%, varies by location and team.
3+ years of project/program management experience, preferably in a fast-paced or dynamic environment.
Availability to work in-office/on-site at our Warsaw location(s) five days a week.
Fluency in Polish.
Life at Palantir
We want every Palantirian to achieve their best outcomes, that’s why we celebrate individuals’ strengths, skills, and interests, from your first interview to your longterm growth, rather than rely on traditional career ladders. Paying attention to the needs of our community enables us to optimize our opportunities to grow and helps ensure many pathways to success at Palantir. Promoting health and well-being across all areas of Palantirians’ lives is just one of the ways we’re investing in our community. Learn more at Life at Palantir and note that our offerings may vary by region.
In keeping consistent with Palantir’s values and culture, we believe employees are “better together” and in-person work affords the opportunity for more creative outcomes. Therefore, we encourage employees to work from our offices to foster connectivity and innovation. Many teams do offer hybrid options (WFH a day or two a week), allowing our employees to strike the right trade-off for their personal productivity. Based on business need, there are a few roles that allow for “Remote” work on an exceptional basis. If you are applying for one of these roles, you must work from the city and or country in which you are employed. If the posting is specified as Onsite, you are required to work from an office.
If you want to empower the world's most important institutions, you belong here. Palantir values excellence regardless of background. We are committed to making the application and hiring process accessible to everyone and will provide a reasonable accommodation for those living with a disability. If you need an accommodation for the application or hiring process, please reach out and let us know how we can help.
Please note that you will never be asked to submit a payment or share financial information to participate in our interview process. If you suspect that you've been contacted by a scammer, we recommend you cease all communication with the individual and consider reporting them to the relevant authorities, such as the US FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3).
Firma, która zmienia świat
W Palantirze dążymy do rozwiązywania najważniejszych i najbardziej wymagających problemów na świecie. Wierzymy, że tworzenie oprogramowania umożliwiającego podejmowanie decyzji przez ludzi jest najlepszym sposobem na osiągnięcie tego celu. Współpracujemy z najważniejszymi instytucjami na świecie, aby przeobrazić sposób, w jaki wykorzystują dane i technologię. Nasze oprogramowanie było wykorzystywane m.in. do powstrzymania ataków terrorystycznych, odkrywania nowych leków, zdobywania przewagi na globalnych rynkach finansowych.
Rola
Jako analityk ds. niezawodności systemów i operacji jesteś siłą napędową wdrożeń rozwiązań Palantir. Odpowiadasz za opracowywanie, wdrażanie i realizację procesów, które usprawniają przepływ pracy i ograniczają utrudnienia. Monitorujesz projekty i dbasz o ich stabilny przebieg, usuwasz przeszkody oraz przewidujesz potrzeby klientów, aby nasi inżynierowie mogli skupić się na rozwiązywaniu problemów technicznych, do których są najlepiej przygotowani.
Na tym stanowisku potrzebne są umiejętności zarządzania projektami, optymalizacji procesów i skutecznej realizacji zadań. Lubisz rozwiązywać problemy i zawsze stawiasz na najlepszy pomysł, nawet jeśli nie jest Twój.
Podstawowe obowiązki
Zajmuj się różnorodnymi problemami i wyzwaniami. Jednego dnia możesz wspierać wdrożenie u dużego klienta o globalnym zasięgu, a już następnego lecieć na drugi koniec świata, aby pomóc przy nowym projekcie pilotażowym.
Bądź jedną z pierwszych osób, które reagują, gdy pojawiają się problemy. Nawet jeśli problem wykracza poza obszar Twojej specjalizacji, podejmujesz inicjatywę, by go rozwiązać, i prosisz o pomoc dopiero wtedy, gdy wyczerpiemy wszystkie dostępne nam możliwości.
Opracowuj i wdrażaj procesy, które ograniczają utrudnienia i pozwalają wszystkim członkom zespołu skupić się na tym, co robią najlepiej.
Myśl kreatywnie, współpracuj i rób wszystko, aby wykonać zadanie.
Co sobie cenimy
Wyjątkowa umiejętność podejmowania trafnych decyzji i zachowania spokoju pod dużą presją.
Kreatywne podejście do zarządzania projektami oparte na prostych i elastycznych metodykach, które umożliwiają szybkie wprowadzanie kolejnych zmian i bieżące informowanie klientów o postępach bez zbędnych formalności.
Udokumentowane sukcesy w budowaniu owocnych relacji z klientami opartych na współpracy.
Skrupulatna dbałość o szczegóły, w tym prowadzenie dokładnej dokumentacji i staranne śledzenie kluczowych wskaźników projektu.
Zaangażowanie w pracę na miejscu u klientów i/lub we wspieranie projektów wewnętrznych oraz kadry kierowniczej wyższego szczebla, połączone z umiejętnością wprowadzania ładu i usprawniania kluczowych inicjatyw wewnętrznych.
Wymagania
Gotowość do podróży służbowych obejmujących 25–75% czasu pracy (w zależności od lokalizacji i zespołu).
Co najmniej 3 lata doświadczenia w zarządzaniu projektami lub programami, najlepiej w dynamicznym środowisku o szybkim tempie pracy.
Gotowość do pracy stacjonarnej w naszej lokalizacji (lokalizacjach) w Warszawie przez pięć dni w tygodniu.
Biegła znajomość języka polskiego.
Życie w Palantir
Chcemy, aby każdy z pracowników firmy Palantir osiągał najlepsze wyniki, dlatego doceniamy mocne strony, umiejętności i zainteresowania poszczególnych osób od pierwszej rozmowy kwalifikacyjnej po długoterminowy rozwój, zamiast polegać na tradycyjnych drabinach kariery. Zwracanie uwagi na potrzeby naszej społeczności umożliwia nam optymalizację naszych możliwości rozwoju i pomaga zapewnić wiele dróg do sukcesu w Palantir. Promowanie zdrowia i dobrego samopoczucia we wszystkich obszarach życia pracowników firmy Palantir to tylko jeden ze sposobów inwestowania w naszą społeczność. Dowiedz się więcej na stronie Życie w Palantir i pamiętaj, że nasza oferta może się różnić w zależności od regionu.
Wierzymy, że postępowanie zgodne z wartościami i kulturą firmy Palantir sprawia, iż pracownicy „lepiej współpracują ze sobą”, a praca osobista daje możliwość bardziej kreatywnych wyników. Dlatego zachęcamy pracowników do pracy w naszych biurach, aby wspierać powiązania i innowacje. Wiele zespołów oferuje opcje hybrydowe (dzień lub dwa dni w tygodniu pracy w domu), dzięki czemu nasi pracownicy mogą uzyskać odpowiedni kompromis dla swojej osobistej produktywności. W zależności od potrzeb biznesowych istnieje kilka ról, które pozwalają na „zdalną” pracę w wyjątkowych warunkach. Jeśli ubiegasz się o jedno z tych stanowisk, musisz pracować w mieście i kraju, w którym jesteś zatrudniony. Jeśli oferta w ogłoszeniu jest określona jako „na miejscu”, musisz pracować w biurze.
Jeśli chcesz wzmacniać najważniejsze instytucje świata, jest to miejsce dla Ciebie. Palantir ceni doskonałość niezależnie od pochodzenia. Dokładamy wszelkich starań, aby proces składania aplikacji i zatrudniania był dostępny dla wszystkich, oraz zapewnimy odpowiednie udogodnienia osobom z niepełnosprawnością. Jeśli potrzebujesz udogodnienia w procesie aplikacji lub zatrudnienia, skontaktuj się z nami i daj nam znać, jak możemy Ci pomóc.
Pamiętaj, że nigdy nie zostaniesz poproszony(-a) o przesłanie płatności lub udostępnienie informacji finansowych w celu wzięcia udziału w naszym procesie rekrutacyjnym. Jeśli podejrzewasz, że skontaktował się z Tobą oszust, zalecamy zaprzestanie wszelkiej komunikacji z taką osobą i rozważenie zgłoszenia jej odpowiednim organom, takim jak amerykańskie Centrum ds. Zgłaszania Przestępstw Internetowych (IC3) przy FBI (FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3).

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
The Startup SaaS Platform team works with European startups in one of Stripe’s must-win segments. At Stripe, SaaS platforms are companies using Stripe to embed payments and make financial services a core part of their own product, meaning their partnership with Stripe is vital to the success of their business, like Shopify, Mindbody and Lightspeed.
Saas platforms are a global priority for Stripe, and our team identifies and develops Stripe’s future strategic accounts in Europe. We identify and partner with high-potential companies, helping them leverage Stripe’s solutions to better support their users, scale globally and create new revenue streams through monetisation of payments and financial services.
What you’ll do
We’re hiring for new business acquisition account executive roles on this team. As a Hunter Account Executive on the Startup SaaS Platforms team, you will be instrumental in driving Stripe’s future growth.
The role will involve prospecting to businesses, articulating the value of Stripe and strategically advising them on how they can optimize their business using Stripe solutions, either for the first time or by expanding their use of Stripe. We guide our users to understand how Stripe’s modern financial infrastructure can support their growth ambitions and provide a competitive edge for their business or product.
Responsibilities
Directly manage and cultivate a named account list while crafting comprehensive account plans aimed at winning and expanding business with high growth Startups.
Understand users' pain points and our value to SaaS platform clients, proposing tailored solutions that consult on their long-term growth.
Bring clients through complex evaluations of Stripe’s solutions for SaaS platforms, working with cross-functional teams (Product, Risk, Operations) to successfully follow new projects through to activation and adoption.
Foster relationships with executive stakeholders and represent Stripe in the local startup ecosystem to help us grow our footprint and mindshare.
Work with other cross-functional teams on shared initiatives to drive internal efficiency and broader business impact.
Who you are
We’re looking for a curious, well-rounded strategic seller who can build strong relationships with Stripe’s clients in this key segment. You are comfortable owning your territory, balancing a mix of high value, strategic deals with smaller, high velocity deal cycles.
You are solution-oriented, adaptable, have a keen interest in further developing yourself and know how to get things done. You do enjoy a highly-technical sales cycle and working with a diverse group of internal stakeholders.
We’re looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.

Fluent in French and proficient in both written and spoken English
4-6+ years of experience in a client-facing/closing sales role
Ability to understand complex technical ecosystems
Desire to build great relationships, often with technical customers
Analytical ability to manage complex sales cycles
Agile and adaptable individual capable of operating in highly ambiguous and fluid environments, specifically managing high-velocity deal cycles.
Bias towards team success and knowledge sharing
A knack for seeking out and working well with a wide range of stakeholders, both internally and externally
Motivation to operate in a highly ambiguous and fluid environment
Preferred requirements
Prior experience in a sales and/or customer-facing role at a high growth technology company
Prior experience working directly with or selling to Startups
Prior experience working directly with or selling to portfolio companies of VC funds.
Comfort interpreting and discussing API-first solutions

Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era.
