Okta is looking for an experienced, self-motivated team player who will be a vital component for Okta’s sustainability (1) analytics and reporting, and (2) automation. This person will drive accurate and timely reporting and insights to meet the expectations of external stakeholders (customers, investors, and regulators) and internal stakeholders (executives, program leads, etc). This person will identify opportunities to automate data collection, analytics, reporting, and dashboards across various programs to streamline and simplify this work, and to enable our teams to scale to meet the moment and the growing stakeholder requirements. The role will drive impact as reporting is a customer business requirement, and data insights are foundational to building sustainability programs that drive business value and reduce environmental impacts.
The ideal candidate for this role has 4+ years of data analytics, visualization, reporting, and automation experience, as well as sustainability, GHG accounting and carbon analytics experience. We are looking for highly motivated team players who have strong analytical skills, excellent attention to detail, and the ability to work cross functionally, as well as independently. This is a great opportunity for candidates seeking experience within a dynamic Finance organization deeply embedded in the teams we support.
Job Duties and Responsibilities
Manage reporting, including collect and clean data; maintain accuracy of Okta’s sustainability metrics; analyze, summarize and visualize for external reporting/disclosures - annual GHG Inventory and public language, sustainability section of annual Fact Sheet, CDP, and for internal dashboards and reporting, and supporting regulatory compliance. .
Prepare audit ready data sets and documentation; maintain traceable audit controls and trails; implement any feedback to strengthen internal controls and improve future assurance readiness; and manage third party assurance processes.
Carbon analytics: Own the design and maintenance of models to track and analyze, forecast, and reduce greenhouse gas emissions and environmental impact. Support quantifying the value of this work in terms of cost savings from reducing energy consumption, revenue supported by meeting customer expectations, etc.
Support key teams with related analytics and reporting, such as
IR team with investor ESG questionnaires/surveys and data analysis and visualization for investor disclosures and presentations.
Finance SEC Accounting, Legal, & Sustainability teams with sustainability data for SEC and other public disclosures.
Finance to integrate and maintain sustainability metrics in T&E dashboard, and to conduct analysis and provide recommendations and visualizations for each team/org.
Workplace team with building & utility sustainability data and analytics
Finance, Technology, Data & Insights (TDI), and Sustainability teams to understand AI usage and environmental impacts, and identify opportunities to use AI more efficiently. Also partner with Engineering and Product teams to turn environmental data insights into actionable impact.
Design, build, automate and maintain user-friendly dashboards, analytical tools, and reports to track metrics and performance indicators, and prepare slides and briefs, such as
Quarterly sustainability metrics/dashboards for Okta’s Executive Committee (CFO, CLO, COO) and Board of Directors.
Quarterly sustainability metrics in Okta for Good (O4G) dashboard and QBR to streamline and simplify quarterly internal reporting for the team.
Quarterly vendor engagement on sustainability metrics to support Okta Climate Lead and Procurement to make progress on our science-based target for vendor engagement
Identify and implement additional opportunities to automate, streamline and simplify. Build and “manage” “digital worker/agent(s)”.
Identify and implement opportunities to integrate sustainability data into other reports, dashboards, and processes to inform decision-making.
Assist with additional data requests, reporting, and automation - collection, cleaning, analysis, and visualization to incorporate into short and long-term planning, program design and evaluation, etc. As needed, prepare decks, reports, and briefs.
Understand and apply the Sustainability Accounting Standards Board (SASB) and International Sustainability Standards Board (ISSB) reporting frameworks, as well as Task Force on Climate-Related Financial Disclosures (TCFD), CDP (Carbon Disclosure Project), etc.
Stay abreast of sustainability reporting regulations and trends, including the ESG ratings and rankings ecosystem, reporting frameworks, sustainability reporting standards and disclosure legislation, and provide recommendations for how these developments should inform Okta’s non-financial reporting practices.
Cross-collaborate effectively with partner organizations
Leverage and improve current systems to increase productivity and accuracy
Assist with and manage frequent ad hoc projects to support management and business decisions
What you can bring:
Sustainability and/or GHG accounting experience and/or regulatory reporting and third party assurance (required)
Masters degree in sustainability and/or GHG accounting (preferred)
4+ years experience data analytics, visualization, reporting, and automation
AI literacy (required)
Must demonstrate analytical prowess, strategic thinking and a command of the details
Excellent modeling and analysis skills
Experience with Tableau and Salesforce
Advanced Excel and PowerPoint skills are required
Experience with other software programs like Watershed (GHG inventory accounting software), Coupa, Netsuite, Workday (preferred)
Ability to manage multiple tasks in a deadline-driven environment while maintaining a high level of precision
Exceptional cross functional project management skills; ability to bring people together around a common goal and lead by influence. Highly skilled in building smart and efficient cross-functional processes
Excellent verbal and written communication skills, experience presenting analyses to management
Ability to work as a team player in a cooperative environment, as well as effective working independently with minimal supervision
Adept at synthesizing complex inputs from multiple stakeholders
Working knowledge of broadly recognized and emerging ESG reporting frameworks and standards, such as The GHG Protocol, SBTi, CDP, and TCFD (preferred)
A desire to build
Positive, can do attitude
A proactive and curious mindset with a strong desire to learn and experiment with new technologies, especially in the realm of AI.

Identity Threat Protection with Okta AI

Deep expertise in Java, including a strong understanding of object-oriented design, multithreading, and building high-performance services.

- **Location:** Dublin, Ireland  (also: Ireland Locations)
- **Department:** 4900 Support Eng & PM
- **Link:** https://stripe.com/jobs/search?gh_jid=8143563

Who we are
About Stripe
Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. Millions of companies—from the world’s largest enterprises to the most ambitious startups—use Stripe to accept payments, grow their revenue, and accelerate new business opportunities. Our mission is to increase the GDP of the internet, and we have a staggering amount of work ahead. That means you have an unprecedented opportunity to put the global economy within everyone’s reach while doing the most important work of your career.
About the team
Support Technologies builds the products, platforms, and capabilities behind Stripe’s support experiences. The team helps users resolve issues quickly, supports internal teams, and creates reusable technology that improves support across Stripe.
This role focuses on the complex support needs of Platforms and Connect. Platforms, connected accounts, and their customers each have different relationships with Stripe, access levels, and responsibilities. Resolving an issue often requires understanding who owns the account, which permissions apply, how products are configured, and which party should provide support.
The team is building the foundations for more contextual support across this multi-party ecosystem. A key part of the work is improving Stripe’s customer-facing AI agent so it can understand these relationships, handle complex cases, and provide accurate, personalized resolutions at scale.
What you’ll do
As a Product Manager for Support Experience, you will be responsible for improving the outcomes of Stripe’s user-facing AI agent that holds 100k+ conversations per week. You’ll be at the forefront of applied AI, solving problems for businesses and consumers, and redefining what great can look like. You’ll directly build agent capabilities to solve real-world user problems. That means getting hands-on with prompts, evals, skills, tools and the machinery that lets you introspect and identify opportunities within the conversational experience.
Responsibilities
Develop the product strategy and roadmap for Support Technologies serving Platform and Connect use cases.
Build a deep understanding of the needs of platform operators, connected accounts, Stripe support teams, and platform support teams.
Improve the capabilities and effectiveness of Stripe’s user-facing AI agent for Platform and Connect support experiences.
Identify recurring support problems and translate them into reusable product, platform, and AI agent capabilities.
Build, ship, and iterate on the prompts, skills, tools, and evaluations that enable the AI agent to solve real-world user problems.
Improve how account relationships, roles, permissions, product configurations, and support history inform routing and resolution across AI and human-assisted support.
Build tools that help the AI agent and support teams understand complex Platform and Connect issues and take the right next action.
Enable platforms to support their connected accounts more effectively through appropriate tooling, visibility, automation, and contextual guidance.
Help clarify whether Stripe, the platform, or another party is responsible for resolving a user’s issue, and route the issue accordingly.
Create continuous improvement loops that turn insights from AI agent conversations and support interactions into scaled solutions.
Partner with Platform, Connect, and conversational AI product teams to incorporate support capabilities into critical product journeys.
Who you are
We’re looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply.
Minimum requirements
7+ years of product management experience, excluding internships and co-ops.
Deep user empathy and a high bar for user experience.
Experience leading cross-team initiatives from ideation through execution.
Experience leading technical products or a background in computer science or a similar field.
Strong written and verbal communication skills.

## 81. Site Reliability Operations Analyst - Legal

- **Key:** `lever/palantir/4a9e0649-a925-4b25-914d-1eea058a3e01`
- **Location:** Washington, D.C.
- **Department:** Legal
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/palantir/4a9e0649-a925-4b25-914d-1eea058a3e01

A World-Changing Company
Palantir builds the world’s leading software for data-driven decisions and operations. By bringing the right data to the people who need it, our platforms empower our partners to develop lifesaving drugs, forecast supply chain disruptions, locate missing children, and more.
Core Responsibilities
Partner with leaders to clarify priorities, frame problems, define outcomes, and sequence important initiatives.
Establish and run a lightweight operating rhythm, including planning sessions, team reviews, agendas, and pre-reads. Track decisions, action items, milestones, and commitments, and provide concise updates that surface risks early and clarify next steps.
Drive cross-functional initiatives from planning through completion. Align stakeholders, milestones, dependencies, and decisions so work continues to move.
Prepare leaders for decisions with concise briefings that clarify context, options, tradeoffs, open questions, and required decisions.
When a critical initiative goes off track, quickly establish the facts, identify an owner and path to resolution, coordinate the response, and keep stakeholders informed.
Anticipate team needs, remove friction, and manage critical handoffs across groups.
Improve processes, workflows, and tools based on recurring pain points and lessons learned.
What We Value
Strong judgment and composure when priorities shift or high-stakes issues arise.
The ability to build trust and communicate clearly with leaders, peers, and partner teams.
A lightweight, creative approach to program management that brings clarity without adding unnecessary process.
Meticulous attention to detail, including accurate records and diligent tracking of decisions, commitments, and key metrics.
Proactive ownership, resourcefulness, and a willingness to make the first move when work is blocked.
Discretion and care when handling sensitive information.
What We Require
Three or more years of experience in project or program management, business operations, executive operations, chief of staff work, or a related role, preferably in a fast-moving environment.
Experience coordinating complex initiatives across teams and following work through to measurable outcomes.
Strong written and verbal communication, including the ability to prepare clear, concise materials.
Ability to manage competing priorities, bring structure to ambiguity, and make progress with limited direction.
Sound judgment and discretion when working with confidential or sensitive information.
Prior work on a legal team or compliance team is a plus.
Salary
The estimated salary range for this position is estimated to be $93,000 - $160,000/year. Total compensation for this position may also include Restricted Stock units, sign-on bonus and other potential future incentives. Further note that total compensation for this position will be determined by each individual’s relevant qualifications, work experience, skills, and other factors. This estimate excludes the value of any potential sign-on bonus; the value of any benefits offered; and the potential future value of any long-term incentives.
Our benefits aim to promote health and wellbeing across all areas of Palantirians’ lives. We work to continuously improve our offerings and listen to our community as we design and update them. The list below details our available benefits and some of the perks that can be enjoyed as an employee of Palantir Technologies.
Benefits
• Employees (and their eligible dependents) can enroll in medical, dental, and vision insurance as well as voluntary life insurance
• Employees are automatically covered by Palantir’s basic life, AD&D and disability insurance
• Commuter benefits
• Relocation assistance
• Take what you need paid time off, not accrual based
• 2 weeks paid time off built into the end of each year (subject to team and business needs)
• 10 paid holidays throughout the calendar year
• Supportive leave of absence program including time off for military service and medical events
• Paid leave for new parents and subsidized back-up care for all parents
• Fertility and family building benefits including but not limited to adoption, surrogacy, and preservation
• Stipend to help with expenses that come with a new child
• Employees can enroll in Palantir’s 401k plan
Life at Palantir
We want every Palantirian to achieve their best outcomes, that’s why we celebrate individuals’ strengths, skills, and interests, from your first interview to your longterm growth, rather than rely on traditional career ladders. Paying attention to the needs of our community enables us to optimize our opportunities to grow and helps ensure many pathways to success at Palantir. Promoting health and well-being across all areas of Palantirians’ lives is just one of the ways we’re investing in our community. Learn more at Life at Palantir and note that our offerings may vary by region.
In keeping consistent with Palantir’s values and culture, we believe employees are “better together” and in-person work affords the opportunity for more creative outcomes. Therefore, we encourage employees to work from our offices to foster connectivity and innovation. Many teams do offer hybrid options (WFH a day or two a week), allowing our employees to strike the right trade-off for their personal productivity. Based on business need, there are a few roles that allow for “Remote” work on an exceptional basis. If you are applying for one of these roles, you must work from the state in which you are employed. If the posting is specified as Onsite, you are required to work from an office.
If you want to empower the world's most important institutions, you belong here. Palantir values excellence regardless of background. We are proud to be an Equal Opportunity Employer for all, including but not limited to Veterans and those with disabilities. Palantir is committed to making the application and hiring process accessible to everyone and will provide a reasonable accommodation for those living with a disability. If you need an accommodation for the application or hiring process, please reach out and let us know how we can help.
Please note that you will never be asked to submit a payment or share financial information to participate in our interview process. If you suspect that you've been contacted by a scammer, we recommend you cease all communication with the individual and consider reporting them to the relevant authorities, such as the US FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3).
If you would like to understand more about how your personal data will be processed by Palantir, please see our Privacy Policy.

## 82. Analyst - Computer Software Validation (Remote)

- **Key:** `lever/veeva/8daa7569-ea8e-4ea6-9778-23d2181be916`
- **Location:** North Carolina - Raleigh
- **Department:** Professional Services
- **Link:** https://jobs.lever.co/veeva/8daa7569-ea8e-4ea6-9778-23d2181be916

Veeva Systems is a mission-driven organization and pioneer in industry cloud, helping life sciences companies bring therapies to patients faster. As one of the fastest-growing SaaS companies in history, we surpassed $3B in revenue in our last fiscal year with extensive growth potential ahead.
At the heart of Veeva are our values: Do the Right Thing, Customer Success, Employee Success, and Speed. We're not just any public company – we made history in 2021 by becoming a public benefit corporation (PBC), legally bound to balancing the interests of customers, employees, society, and investors.
As a Work Anywhere company, we support your flexibility to work from home or in the office, so you can thrive in your ideal environment.
Join us in transforming the life sciences industry, committed to making a positive impact on its customers, employees, and communities.
The Role
We are looking for a Computer Software Validation (CSV) professional with a specialty in clinical trial software. This role would give you the opportunity to work on a leading solution in Randomization and Trial Supply Management (RTSM) software and help our customers bring treatments to patients faster. To excel in this role you will require strong technical writing capabilities and the ability to simplify complex topics.
As a member of the RTSM team, you will work closely with Development and Project Management teams to support customer RTSM implementations in a fast-paced environment. You will use your expertise to take ownership of the validation process for assigned projects and play an important role in building and maintaining product excellence for the RTSM system.
This is a remote role with Veeva's Work Anywhere policy allowing you to work in an office or at home on any given day. It's about getting the work done in the way and place that works best for each person.
What You'll Do
Participate in RTSM requirement gathering sessions with clients and RTSM team members
Closely collaborate with RTSM Development and Project Management teams to ensure requirements are defined in a clear, testable, and compliant format
Ensure requirements are adequately tested following a risk-based approach
Develop and maintain CSV deliverables including requirements specifications, test scripts, summary reports, and trace matrices
Manage Validation testing cycles and resolution of issues and enhancement requests
Maintain change control documentation to ensure ongoing validation of the RTSM system
Provide technical expertise to RTSM team members in computer system validation, applicable regulations, and other quality system requirements
Support customers as needed in RTSM validation activities
Requirements
1+ year of experience in a CSV for clinical trial software (CTMS, CDMS, eTMF, etc.)
Working knowledge of software development cycle (SDLC)
Working knowledge of GCP Guidance such as 21 CFR Part 11, EU Annex 11
Exceptional attention to detail
Good interpersonal skills and ability to communicate in English with team members and customers of varying roles and skill levels
Nice to Have
Familiarity with the conduct of clinical trials
Previous experience with RTSM
Expert in Microsoft Word and Excel
Interviewing with Veeva
We value your time and believe in a transparent hiring process. Here is the process you can expect.
Follow the application process and submit your resume.
Within 3 days, you will receive a link to a personality assessment administered by a third party, unless you have already completed it with Veeva previously.
Once you complete the assessment, our team will review your full application package and follow up via email with our decision.
If moving to the interview stage, the process is as follows:
A conversation with the hiring manager
A practical case exercise
A final conversation with our group's Senior Leader.
Once all interviews are complete, the manager will be in touch with a final decision.
Perks & Benefits
Medical, dental, vision, and basic life insurance
Flexible PTO and company paid holidays
Retirement programs
1% charitable giving program
Compensation
Base pay: $60,000 - $100,000
The salary range listed here has been provided to comply with local regulations and represents a potential base salary range for this role. Please note that actual salaries may vary within the range above or below, depending on experience and location. We look at compensation for each individual and base our offer on your unique qualifications, experience, and expected contributions. This position may also be eligible for other types of compensation in addition to base salary, such as variable bonus and/or stock bonus.

Stripe is a financial infrastructure platform for businesses. About the team
Risk Operations is responsible for managing the risk relationships spanning across global payment method, card, and financial partners. It's our mission to build operational controls to mitigate user and partner risk and enable strategic development opportunities.
What you'll do
As a Risk Operations Analyst, you'll work to ensure our policies, operations, and workflows align to the needs of our financial partners. You'll be accountable for workflows impacting a variety of cross-functional internal and external stakeholders to identify and assess regulatory, contractual, and partner risk. Further, you'll influence and advise operational teams in building out scalable operational processes to ensure compliance with service level agreements, routine reporting, and overall risk mitigation.
Responsibilities
Demonstrate sound judgment and an ability to navigate non-binary, high-risk decisions
Evaluate merchants, from successful venture-backed startups to well-established institutions by assessing suitability to process on Stripe
Adhere closely to process while still constantly questioning assumptions, suggesting improvements, and following up on implementation of those improvements
Effectively and clearly communicate, including with other Stripe employees, Stripe users, and Stripe financial partners
Identify trends and further report these to cross-functional teams to enhance detection systems
Contribute to projects to enhance and optimize due diligence strategy systems and tools
Help build and scale processes across the risk operations ecosystem
Identify gaps in current systems, policies, and strategies, and recommend enhancements and process improvements to mitigate emerging risks through a collaborative approach with stakeholders
Who you are
We're looking for someone who meets the minimum requirements to be considered for the role. If you meet these requirements, you are encouraged to apply. The preferred qualifications are a bonus, not a requirement.
Minimum requirements
5+ years of experience in risk management, compliance, or operations in a fast-moving environment
Experience scaling operational processes and driving quantifiable improvements
Experience working cross-functionally on complex problems
Ability to use data to quantify problems, tell a story, and influence a solution
The ability and desire to challenge the status quo
Creative thinker who considers problems from a customer perspective while being pragmatic and solutions-oriented when solving them
Experience analyzing feedback patterns and using those insights to drive process and tooling improvements
You have a lot of ownership, are accountable, and drive towards ambitious goals.
Deep empathy for entrepreneurs running a business and motivation to solve problems to empower them
Preferred qualifications
Experience with risk assessment and mitigation strategies in a financial technology environment
Knowledge of payment processing systems and associated risks

## 86. Staff Product Designer - UI Platform

- **Key:** `greenhouse/okta/8326116`
- **Location:** Bellevue, Washington; Chicago, Illinois; New York, New York; Toronto, Ontario, Canada; Washington, DC (also: San Francisco, CA, United States)
- **Department:** Product Design & UXR-650
- **Link:** https://www.okta.com/company/careers/opportunity/8326116?gh_jid=8326116

Secure Every Identity, from AI to Human
Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era. This work requires a relentless drive to solve complex challenges with real-world stakes. We are looking for builders and owners who operate with speed and urgency and execute with excellence.
This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
The team
The UI Platform team is the force multiplier for Okta’s entire product portfolio. By building world-class components, accessible frameworks, and developer-friendly tools, your work will directly impact millions of administrative and end-user interactions every day. You will work alongside dedicated product and engineering partners in an environment that values speed, clarity, and career-defining impact.
The role
The Staff Product Designer – UI Platform is a high-impact individual contributor role, serving as a foundational design lead for Okta's global user experience infrastructure. Joining the Product Platform group, you will act as a resident expert and strategic thought partner, shaping the horizontal design patterns, design architecture, localization/globalization frameworks, and accessibility standards (WCAG) that power every Okta Workforce Identity product.
You will partner directly with cross functional UI Platform leads to drive platform-level initiatives, harmonizing user experience (UX) and developer experience (DevEx) across our engineering and product organizations. You will also collaborate closely with the Design Systems team, acting as a bridge between the system and feature teams applying it to our product.
What you’ll be doing
When everyone on the team can generate UI, the designer's role shifts. You become the person responsible for strong systems thinking, taste, quality, experience, and being the voice of the customer. You hold the quality bar when speed is pushing everything toward "fine."
UI & system leadership: In partnership with the Design Systems team, serve as a UI platform and design system expert, identifying / creating, documenting, and evangelizing scalable, accessible (WCAG), and localized patterns to resolve dependencies across products.
Strategic partnership: Partner with cross functional UI Platform leads to align vision with design strategy, co-owning the roadmap to balance features, tech debt, quality, and adoption.
Raise the craft: Enable Okta Research and Development teams through clear alignment and documentation. Improve org-wide processes and mentor designers to promote craft excellence, candid feedback, and growth.
Data informed decision making: Leverage data and AI insights to inform clear, platform-level design decisions and streamline governance.
Who we are looking for
You are proficient in AI workflows and how to apply them to the product design process, along with opinions about where AI tools help and where they fall short
You are passionate about helping customers solve real problems and upholding design UX principles to help teams deliver cohesive and quality user experiences
You relish effective teamwork – and you are a key contributor to building a healthy team
You bring a systems-thinking lens to the conversation and can effectively identify elegant, extensible solutions
You have excellent craft and enjoy diving into the details to ensure high quality experiences for our customers, while providing design feedback and mentorship to fellow designers
You have a learning mindset, able to manage ambiguity with openness and curiosity, helping your team drive toward clarity
You are someone who sees design as a partner in shaping strategic and operational product and UX outcomes; comfortable owning all stages of design for multiple teams within a given product area
You have strong communication skills and can distill complex concepts into simple, easy to understand narratives, while advocating for consistent, high quality design based on sound rationale
Your portfolio will show that you've worked on and led complex and challenging projects across all aspects of the design process (conceptual design, interaction design, information architecture, and visual design) across large, horizontal scopes and groups, with a preference for large-scale enterprise and SAAS products
You share our values, and work in accordance with those values
Qualifications
Degree in a related field or relevant work experience as a Product Designer
Proficient in Figma, Miro, Balsamiq and / or similar design tools
Possesses knowledge of HTML / CSS / JavaScript
10+ years of experience as Product Designer
Note for when applying
In your application, please include a link to your portfolio with password (if protected). We prioritize a review of your design craft and any examples of your work in code.
You must attend in person orientation in either Chicago or San Francisco. You must go into your local office 2 days a week.

About the team
This role is open to remote candidates located anywhere within the Eastern Time Zone. Global Payouts empowers Stripe users to move money effortlessly, quickly, and securely to anyone, anywhere. Businesses rely on us to distribute billions of dollars to employees, contractors, sellers, and claimants across our Dashboard, APIs, and platform integrations via Financial Accounts.We are gaining rapid momentum across both self-serve and enterprise segments. Our demand spans high-growth fintechs, leading AI platforms, and some of the world's largest marketplaces.
We are now entering a decisive phase of expansion. Over the next 12–18 months, the capabilities we build robust cross-border infrastructure, advanced enterprise controls, and tailored vertical-specific features will determine whether Global Payouts becomes the definitive default for businesses distributing money at scale. This role sits at the absolute center of that mission.
What you'll do
We are looking for an entrepreneurial Product Manager to spearhead our cross-border expansion and enterprise capabilities. This is a high-impact, broad-scope role where you will set the strategic vision and drive execution across two pillars essential to our hyper-growth:
Cross-border: You will expand the reach and competitive edge of our cross-border infrastructure by building out rate locking, local bank account coverage, and cross-border card rails. Crucially, you will lead the productization of fee passthrough—translating a bespoke solution originally built for a major AI platform into a broadly available, highly scalable capability.
Enterprise: You will define and deliver the advanced controls, reporting, and billing features required by sophisticated, high-volume operators. This includes recipient tax reporting, two-step confirmation workflows, enterprise billing models (e.g., IC+, platform fees), and robust security access controls.
Across both domains, you will partner directly with Sales, Partnerships, and Go-to-Market (GTM) teams to convert active enterprise demand into durable product investments, developing industry-specific capabilities for key verticals like remittance and fintech.
Responsibilities
Cross-Border Expansion
Own the roadmap for global payout infrastructure, prioritizing rate locking, local bank coverage, and cross-border card rails.
Productize fee passthrough, transforming a custom integration into a scalable, self-serve offering to drive rapid adoption among new users.
Design industry-specific solutions for high-value verticals (e.g., remittance, fintech) rooted in deep user research and strategic GTM alignment.
Enterprise Capabilities
Ship enterprise-grade controls and reporting tools, including recipient tax reporting, two-step confirmation workflows, and advanced security access controls.
Architect enterprise billing primitives—such as Interchange Plus (IC+) and platform fees—to enable flexible, scalable commercial models for large payout operators.
Cross-Functional Leadership
Partner with Sales and Partnerships to support active enterprise deals and PUA requests, distilling bespoke client requirements into scalable, prioritized product investments.
Collaborate seamlessly across Financial Accounts, Connect, and the broader money movement platform to ensure new capabilities integrate cleanly and compound in value over time.
Define and track success metrics, holding yourself accountable to measurable adoption targets across both self-serve and enterprise segments.
Who you are
This role is open to remote candidates located anywhere within the Eastern Time Zone.
Minimum requirements
7+ years of product management experience
Demonstrated ability to own a product area end-to-end—defining strategy, driving execution, and measuring outcomes
Experience shipping products that serve multiple user segments or operate across significant technical or regulatory complexity
Proven ability to work with Sales, GTM, or enterprise customers and translate their needs into scalable product investments
Strong analytical instincts: comfort with data, metrics, and building the measurement framework before you ship
Strong written communication: crisp specs, clear PRDs, and the ability to build alignment across functions and levels
Preferred qualifications
Experience in payments, fintech, financial infrastructure, or other regulated industries
Background working on enterprise or B2B products with complex commercial or compliance requirements
Experience productizing bespoke or custom solutions into broadly available features

What you’ll be doing
Partner with peers and leaders in Sales, TAM, Customer Success, Product, Engineering and Program Management to develop scalable measurement strategies for our data assets, products and services
Drive scalable, semantic data models that can support stakeholders both inside of Auth0 and across the enterprise

A World-Changing Company
Palantir builds the world’s leading software for data-driven decisions and operations. By bringing the right data to the people who need it, our platforms empower our partners to develop lifesaving drugs, forecast supply chain disruptions, locate missing children, and more.
The Role
Substrate is the team responsible for Palantir’s core production infrastructure — 100s of K8s clusters — from on-prem to the major cloud hyperscalers, whether they are internet-connected or air-gapped, small hardware footprint or large. As a Senior Software Engineer on Substrate, you will design and build Palantir’s managed Kubernetes product offerings across all these environments.
You and your team will be responsible for bootstrapping and operating the entire fleet of K8s clusters with zero manual steps by building industry leading tooling and contributing to core CNCF components. You will also be responsible for ensuring scale, stability and security across a matrix of compliance regimes and hosting infrastructure types. Your team culture emphasizes engineering rigor and operational excellence at scale. This means issues in production should be pre-empted and deeply root-caused, and investments in automation and self-healing systems are key.
If you’re excited about infrastructure at scale and working with Kubernetes, this is the right role for you.
Core Responsibilities
Deliver a container runtime to challenging new environment types - new clouds, on premise, edge devices
Build automation and establish standards for operating K8s securely at scale with zero manual ops overhead
Drive innovation through adoption of novel K8s features and CNCF tools, making upstream contributions as needed
Design the next generation of Palantir’s infrastructure through a deep understanding of internal systems and CNCF standards
What We Value
Systems programming experience with strong proficiency in golang, C/C++ or equivalent
Working knowledge or hands on experience of infrastructure automation tools such as Terraform, ansible, puppet or K8s operators, and competent coding in Go, Java, or equivalent for the purposes of automation or scripting
Deep familiarity with hardware and OS configurations, diagnostic tooling, networking nuts and bolts
Deep familiarity with containers (Docker) and orchestration (Kubernetes) at scale
Experience working with a cloud provider (AWS/Azure/GCE), or sysadmin/SRE experience in data centers
Experience designing, building, and operating high-scale observability or infrastructure systems
Working knowledge of networking fundamentals, experience with CNIs or cloud networking infrastructure preferred
What We Require
4+ years of professional software development experience on core infrastructure with emphasis on operational excellence
2+ years of experience contributing to the system design or architecture (architecture, design patterns, reliability and scaling) of new and existing systems
Bachelor's degree in Computer Science or equivalent
Life at Palantir
We want every Palantirian to achieve their best outcomes, that’s why we celebrate individuals’ strengths, skills, and interests, from your first interview to your longterm growth, rather than rely on traditional career ladders. Paying attention to the needs of our community enables us to optimize our opportunities to grow and helps ensure many pathways to success at Palantir. Promoting health and well-being across all areas of Palantirians’ lives is just one of the ways we’re investing in our community. Learn more at Life at Palantir and note that our offerings may vary by region.
In keeping consistent with Palantir’s values and culture, we believe employees are “better together” and in-person work affords the opportunity for more creative outcomes. Therefore, we encourage employees to work from our offices to foster connectivity and innovation. Many teams do offer hybrid options (WFH a day or two a week), allowing our employees to strike the right trade-off for their personal productivity. Based on business need, there are a few roles that allow for “Remote” work on an exceptional basis. If you are applying for one of these roles, you must work from the city and or country in which you are employed. If the posting is specified as Onsite, you are required to work from an office.
If you want to empower the world's most important institutions, you belong here. Palantir values excellence regardless of background. We are committed to making the application and hiring process accessible to everyone and will provide a reasonable accommodation for those living with a disability. If you need an accommodation for the application or hiring process, please reach out and let us know how we can help.
Please note that you will never be asked to submit a payment or share financial information to participate in our interview process. If you suspect that you've been contacted by a scammer, we recommend you cease all communication with the individual and consider reporting them to the relevant authorities, such as the US FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3).

## 92. Senior Internal Auditor

- **Key:** `greenhouse/okta/8345798`
- **Location:** Bengaluru, India (also: Bengaluru)
- **Department:** Internal Audit-135
- **Link:** https://www.okta.com/company/careers/opportunity/8345798?gh_jid=8345798

Secure Every Identity, from AI to Human
Identity is the key to unlocking the potential of AI. Okta secures AI by building the trusted, neutral infrastructure that enables organizations to safely embrace this new era. This work requires a relentless drive to solve complex challenges with real-world stakes. We are looking for builders and owners who operate with speed and urgency and execute with excellence.
This is an opportunity to do career-defining work. We're all in on this mission. If you are too, let's talk.
Get to Know Okta
Okta is The World’s Identity Company. We free everyone to safely use any technology—anywhere, on any device or application. Our Workforce Identity Cloud and Customer Identity Cloud (Auth0) solutions enable secure yet flexible access, authentication, and automation that transforms how people move through the digital world, putting Identity at the heart of business security and growth.
At Okta, we celebrate a variety of perspectives and experiences. We are not looking for someone who checks every single box—we’re looking for lifelong learners and owners who can make us better with their unique experiences, technical acumen, and passion for continuous improvement.
Join our team! We’re building a world where Identity belongs to you.
About the Role
Okta is searching for an experienced, detail-oriented, and high-impact Senior Internal Auditor to join our global Internal Audit team and drive the successful execution of Okta’s global Internal Audit and SOX IT compliance program.
In this role, you will lead and execute IT General Control (ITGC), IT Application Control (ITAC), and Information Produced by Entity (IPE/Key Reports) walkthroughs and testing across Okta’s critical enterprise applications, infrastructure, and cloud environments. You will operate with significant autonomy, collaborate closely with external auditors, mentor and review the work of auditors, and partner with Engineering, Information Security, and Finance stakeholders to safeguard the integrity of internal controls over financial reporting (ICFR).
Key Responsibilities
1. Audit Execution & Testing (ITGC & ITAC)
Independently lead end-to-end SOX IT walkthroughs and execute design (TOD) and operating effectiveness (TOE) testing in strict alignment with Okta’s Internal Audit and SOX methodology.
Assess IT General Controls across all primary domains:
Logical Access & Security: User lifecycle management (provisioning/deprovisioning), privileged access management (PAM), multi-factor authentication (MFA), and segregation of duties (SoD).
Change Management: Source code repository management, deployment approval gates, testing validation, emergency changes, and CI/CD pipeline controls.
Computer Operations & Resiliency: Automated batch job scheduling and monitoring, data backup verification, incident/problem management, and disaster recovery.
Perform rigorous evaluations of automated application controls (ITAC), system-generated reports/queries (IPE), key data integrations, and interface reconciliations.
Review third-party Service Organization Control reports (SOC 1 / SOC 2 Type II) to evaluate complementary user entity controls (CUECs) and determine impacts on in-scope hosted/SaaS systems.
Participate and support Internal Audits planned for the year.
2. Workpaper Quality, Staff Coaching & Methodology
Prepare self-reviewed, audit-ready workpapers and documentation within Okta's GRC platform with clear evidence, testing samples, and audit conclusions requiring minimal editorial revision.
Review staff and associate auditor work products, providing actionable coaching and feedback to elevate overall team quality and audit rigor.
Actively champion SOX methodology improvements, control standardization, and testing efficiencies across the Internal Audit team.
3. Deficiency Evaluation & Stakeholder Advisory
Pinpoint systemic root causes of control breakdowns and bridge technical gaps between engineering realities and compliance expectations—moving beyond symptom identification.
Draft precise, well-contextualized deficiency write-ups and collaborate with control owners (TDI, Security, Engineering, and Finance) to reach alignment on remediation strategies and Compensating Controls.
Track Management Action Plans (MAPs) and perform remediation testing to validate sustained resolution of identified gaps.
Support direct audit coordination with external auditors, effectively addressing queries and facilitating seamless walkthroughs and sample validations.
4. Automation & Continuous Improvement
Identify opportunities to rationalize redundant controls, eliminate manual testing fatigue, and leverage automated system capabilities.
Assist in incorporating data analytics, scripting, and RPA/bot governance to streamline audit sample extraction, configuration inspections, and continuous control monitoring.
Required Qualifications
Education: Bachelor’s or Master’s degree in Management Information Systems (MIS), Computer Science, Information Technology, Accounting, Finance, or related STEM/business discipline.
Experience: 4–6 years of relevant experience in IT audit, SOX compliance, technology risk consulting, or internal controls assurance within a Big Four public accounting firm and/or a publicly traded enterprise (high-growth SaaS / cloud technology company preferred).
Technical Mastery:
Deep functional knowledge of SOX 404 requirements, PCAOB standards, COBIT, NIST CSF, and the COSO internal control framework.
Practical experience testing core enterprise systems, including ERP platforms (e.g., NetSuite, Workday), CRM/Billing systems (Salesforce), Identity & Access Management tools, and cloud environments (AWS, GCP, Azure).
Demonstrated track record evaluating ITGCs, automated application controls, and complex report/query logics (IPE).
Deficiency & Root Cause Analysis: Proven capability in assessing control deficiencies, determining financial statement impact/significance, and formulating corrective actions with process owners.
Communication & Collaboration: Excellent verbal and written English communication skills; demonstrated confidence in facilitating walkthroughs and presenting findings to cross-functional leadership.
Global Team Player: Self-motivated mindset with the ability to operate independently across distributed time zones (including supporting Pacific Time collaboration when necessary).
Preferred Qualifications
Professional certification such as CISA (Certified Information Systems Auditor) strongly preferred; CIA, CISSP, CISM, or CPA is an added advantage.
Direct hands-on experience using modern GRC tools such as Optro.
Familiarity with database query languages (SQL) or data analytics platforms (Alteryx, Snowflake, Tableau) to automate testing validations.
Understanding of modern DevOps environments, cloud security posture, and CI/CD release management tools (e.g., GitHub, Jira).
Okta’s Core Leadership Competencies
Drives Results: Consistently achieves results and meets deliverables with high precision, even under tight SOX filing timelines and shifting priorities.
Demonstrates Self-Awareness (EQ): Solicits feedback, reflects on outcomes, and proactively adapts communication styles across technical engineering teams and non-technical finance leaders.
Develops Talent: Passionate about mentoring junior auditors and sharing domain knowledge to foster a high-performance culture.
Strategic Mindset & Innovation: Looks ahead to emerging compliance challenges (cloud architectures, AI governance, identity-first security) and translates them into proactive internal audit strategies.
